## 📁 Segurança de Arquivos

### 1. Upload Seguro de Arquivos
```python
# ✅ Seguro - Validação de uploads
import os
from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
from django.conf import settings

def validate_file_extension(value):
    """Validate file extension."""
    ext = os.path.splitext(value.name)[1]
    valid_extensions = ['.pdf', '.doc', '.docx', '.jpg', '.jpeg', '.png']
    
    if not ext.lower() in valid_extensions:
        raise ValidationError('Unsupported file extension.')

def validate_file_size(value):
    """Validate file size (max 5MB)."""
    filesize = value.size
    
    if filesize > 5 * 1024 * 1024:  # 5MB
        raise ValidationError('File size exceeds 5MB limit.')

# models.py
class EventAttachment(models.Model):
    """Secure event attachment model."""
    
    event = models.ForeignKey(Event, on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    file = models.FileField(
        upload_to='event_attachments/%Y/%m/%d/',
        validators=[validate_file_extension, validate_file_size]
    )
    uploaded_by = models.ForeignKey('auth.User', on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    uploaded_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    
    def clean(self):
        """Additional validation."""
        # Verificar tipo de conteúdo real do arquivo
        if self.file:
            # Implementar verificação de magic numbers
            pass

# ✅ Seguro - Servir arquivos de forma segura
# views.py
from django.views.decorators.http import require_http_methods
from django.http import HttpResponseForbidden
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required

@login_required
@require_http_methods(["GET"])
def download_attachment(request, attachment_id: int):
    """Secure file download."""
    attachment = get_object_or_404(EventAttachment, pk=attachment_id)
    
    # Verificar permissão
    if not request.user.has_perm('core.view_event', attachment.event):
        return HttpResponseForbidden("You don't have permission to download this file")
    
    # Servir arquivo de forma segura
    response = HttpResponse(attachment.file.read(), content_type='application/octet-stream')
    response['Content-Disposition'] = f'attachment; filename="{attachment.file.name}"'
    response['X-Content-Type-Options'] = 'nosniff'
    
    return response
```

## 🔍 Auditoria e Logging

### 1. Logging de Segurança Estruturado
```python
# ✅ Seguro - Logging de auditoria
import logging
import json
from datetime import datetime
from django.contrib.auth.signals import user_logged_in, user_logged_out
from django.dispatch import receiver

logger = logging.getLogger('security')

@receiver(user_logged_in)
def log_user_login(sender, request, user, **kwargs):
    """Log successful user login."""
    logger.info(
        "User login successful",
        extra={
            'event_type': 'user_login',
            'user_id': user.id,
            'username': user.username,
            'ip_address': get_client_ip(request),
            'user_agent': request.META.get('HTTP_USER_AGENT', ''),
            'timestamp': datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    )

@receiver(user_logged_out)
def log_user_logout(sender, request, user, **kwargs):
    """Log user logout."""
    logger.info(
        "User logout",
        extra={
            'event_type': 'user_logout',
            'user_id': user.id,
            'username': user.username,
            'timestamp': datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    )

def log_security_event(event_type: str, user, request, details: dict = None):
    """Log security events."""
    log_data = {
        'event_type': event_type,
        'user_id': user.id if user else None,
        'username': user.username if user else None,
        'ip_address': get_client_ip(request),
        'user_agent': request.META.get('HTTP_USER_AGENT', ''),
        'timestamp': datetime.now().isoformat()
    }
    
    if details:
        log_data.update(details)
    
    logger.info(json.dumps(log_data))

# Uso em views
def approve_event(request, event_id: int):
    """Approve event with security logging."""
    event = get_object_or_404(Event, pk=event_id)
    
    if not request.user.has_perm('core.can_approve_event', event):
        log_security_event(
            'permission_denied',
            request.user,
            request,
            {
                'action': 'approve_event',
                'event_id': event_id,
                'reason': 'Insufficient permissions'
            }
        )
        raise PermissionDenied("You don't have permission to approve this event")
    
    # Aprovar evento
    event.status = Event.Status.APPROVED
    event.approved_by = request.user
    event.approved_at = timezone.now()
    event.save()
    
    log_security_event(
        'event_approved',
        request.user,
        request,
        {
            'action': 'approve_event',
            'event_id': event.id,
            'event_title': event.title,
            'project': event.project.name
        }
    )
    
    return redirect('event_detail', event_id=event.id)
```

### 2. Auditoria de Mudanças
```python
# ✅ Seguro - Sistema de auditoria
from django.db import models
from django.contrib.contenttypes.fields import GenericForeignKey
from django.contrib.contenttypes.models import ContentType

class AuditLog(models.Model):
    """Audit log model for tracking changes."""
    
    ACTION_CHOICES = [
        ('CREATE', 'Create'),
        ('UPDATE', 'Update'),
        ('DELETE', 'Delete'),
        ('APPROVE', 'Approve'),
        ('REJECT', 'Reject'),
    ]
    
    user = models.ForeignKey('auth.User', on_delete=models.SET_NULL, null=True)
    action = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=ACTION_CHOICES)
    content_type = models.ForeignKey(ContentType, on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    object_id = models.PositiveIntegerField()
    content_object = GenericForeignKey('content_type', 'object_id')
    
    timestamp = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    ip_address = models.GenericIPAddressField(null=True, blank=True)
    user_agent = models.TextField(blank=True)
    
    changes = models.JSONField(default=dict)  # Armazenar mudanças como JSON
    
    class Meta:
        ordering = ['-timestamp']
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=['content_type', 'object_id']),
            models.Index(fields=['user']),
            models.Index(fields=['timestamp']),
        ]

# Signals para auditoria automática
from django.db.models.signals import post_save, post_delete
from django.dispatch import receiver

@receiver(post_save)
def audit_save(sender, instance, created, **kwargs):
    """Audit model saves."""
    if sender in [AuditLog, Session]:  # Evitar loop
        return
    
    action = 'CREATE' if created else 'UPDATE'
    
    # Obter usuário atual (requer middleware)
    user = getattr(instance, '_audit_user', None)
    
    AuditLog.objects.create(
        user=user,
        action=action,
        content_type=ContentType.objects.get_for_model(instance),
        object_id=instance.pk,
        changes={'data': str(instance)}  # Simplificado
    )

@receiver(post_delete)
def audit_delete(sender, instance, **kwargs):
    """Audit model deletions."""
    if sender in [AuditLog, Session]:
        return
    
    user = getattr(instance, '_audit_user', None)
    
    AuditLog.objects.create(
        user=user,
        action='DELETE',
        content_type=ContentType.objects.get_for_model(instance),
        object_id=instance.pk,
        changes={'deleted_data': str(instance)}
    )
```

## 🎯 Visão Geral do Sistema

O Sistema APRENDER é uma plataforma educacional robusta e madura, desenvolvida em Django 5.2, projetada para gerenciar eventos educacionais, formadores, projetos e processos de aprovação. O sistema atende a uma organização complexa com múltiplos setores, hierarquias e fluxos de trabalho.

## 🔄 Fluxos de Negócio Principais

### 1. Sistema de Aprovação de Eventos

O sistema possui um fluxo de aprovação diferenciado baseado no tipo de projeto:

#### Projetos Vinculados à Superintendência (14 projetos)
**Lista**: AMMA, CATAVENTOS, CIRANDAR, ESCREVER COMUNICAR E SER, Lendo e Escrevendo, MIUDEZES, Novo Lendo, TEMA, TRÂNSITO LEGAL (todos), UNI DUNI TÊ

**Fluxo**: 
```
Coordenador → PENDENTE → Superintendência → PRE_AGENDA → Controle → APROVADO
```

#### Projetos Não-Vinculados (13 projetos)
**Lista**: Vidas, ACerta, Brincando e Aprendendo, Fluir das Emoções, IDEB 10, Ler Ouvir e Contar

**Fluxo**:
```
Coordenador → PRE_AGENDA → Controle → APROVADO
```

#### Implementação Técnica
```python
# core/views/solicitacao_views.py
def _requer_aprovacao_superintendencia(self, solicitacao):
    """Verifica se a solicitação requer aprovação da superintendência."""
    if solicitacao.projeto.setor:
        return solicitacao.projeto.setor.vinculado_superintendencia
    return solicitacao.projeto.vinculado_superintendencia

def processar_aprovacao(self, solicitacao):
    """Processa o fluxo de aprovação baseado nas regras de negócio."""
    if self._requer_aprovacao_superintendencia(solicitacao):
        # Fluxo para projetos vinculados à superintendência
        solicitacao.status = 'PENDENTE'
        # Notificar superintendência
        self.notificar_superintendencia(solicitacao)
    else:
        # Fluxo direto para controle
        solicitacao.status = 'PRE_AGENDA'
        # Notificar controle
        self.notificar_controle(solicitacao)
    
    solicitacao.save()
```

# core/models/project.py
from django.db import models
from django.contrib.auth.models import User

class Project(models.Model):
    """Model representing a project in the system."""
    
    name = models.CharField(max_length=100, verbose_name="Nome do Projeto")
    description = models.TextField(blank=True, verbose_name="Descrição")
    sector = models.ForeignKey('Sector', on_delete=models.PROTECT, verbose_name="Setor")
    is_linked_to_superintendency = models.BooleanField(
        default=False, 
        verbose_name="Vinculado à Superintendência"
    )
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    
    class Meta:
        verbose_name = "Projeto"
        verbose_name_plural = "Projetos"
        ordering = ['name']
    
    def __str__(self):
        return self.name
    
    def requires_superintendency_approval(self):
        """Check if this project requires superintendency approval."""
        if self.sector:
            return self.sector.vinculado_superintendencia
        return self.is_linked_to_superintendency
```

### 2. Modelo de Evento
```python
# core/models/event.py
from django.db import models
from django.contrib.auth.models import User
from django.utils import timezone

class Event(models.Model):
    """Model representing an educational event."""
    
    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        DRAFT = 'DRAFT', 'Rascunho'
        PENDING = 'PENDING', 'Pendente'
        PRE_AGENDA = 'PRE_AGENDA', 'Pré-agenda'
        APPROVED = 'APPROVED', 'Aprovado'
        REJECTED = 'REJECTED', 'Rejeitado'
        CANCELLED = 'CANCELLED', 'Cancelado'
    
    title = models.CharField(max_length=200, verbose_name="Título")
    description = models.TextField(verbose_name="Descrição")
    project = models.ForeignKey('Project', on_delete=models.CASCADE, verbose_name="Projeto")
    
    # Datas
    start_date = models.DateTimeField(verbose_name="Data de Início")
    end_date = models.DateTimeField(verbose_name="Data de Término")
    
    # Localização
    location = models.CharField(max_length=300, blank=True, verbose_name="Local")
    is_online = models.BooleanField(default=False, verbose_name="Online")
    
    # Participantes
    max_participants = models.PositiveIntegerField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name="Máximo de Participantes")
    
    # Status e aprovação
    status = models.CharField(
        max_length=20, 
        choices=Status.choices, 
        default=Status.DRAFT,
        verbose_name="Status"
    )
    
    # Relacionamentos
    trainers = models.ManyToManyField(
        'auth.User', 
        related_name='events_as_trainer',
        verbose_name="Formadores"
    )
    municipalities = models.ManyToManyField(
        'Municipality', 
        verbose_name="Municípios"
    )
    
    # Auditoria
    created_by = models.ForeignKey(
        'auth.User', 
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL, 
        null=True,
        related_name='created_events'
    )
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)
    
    # Aprovação
    approved_by = models.ForeignKey(
        'auth.User', 
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL, 
        null=True, 
        blank=True,
        related_name='approved_events'
    )
    approved_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    
    class Meta:
        verbose_name = "Evento"
        verbose_name_plural = "Eventos"
        ordering = ['-start_date']
    
    def __str__(self):
        return self.title
    
    def can_be_approved(self):
        """Check if event can be approved."""
        return self.status in [self.Status.PENDING, self.Status.PRE_AGENDA]
    
    def is_approved(self):
        """Check if event is approved."""
        return self.status == self.Status.APPROVED
    
    def requires_superintendency_approval(self):
        """Check if event requires superintendency approval."""
        return self.project.requires_superintendency_approval()
```

### 3. Modelo de Formador
```python
# trainers/models.py
from django.db import models
from django.contrib.auth.models import User

class Trainer(models.Model):
    """Extended user model for trainers."""
    
    user = models.OneToOneField(User, on_delete=models.CASCADE)
    phone = models.CharField(max_length=20, blank=True)
    bio = models.TextField(blank=True)
    specialties = models.CharField(max_length=500, blank=True)
    
    # Disponibilidade
    is_available = models.BooleanField(default=True, verbose_name="Disponível")
    blocked_until = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True, verbose_name="Bloqueado até")
    
    # Documentação
    document_number = models.CharField(max_length=20, blank=True, verbose_name="Número do Documento")
    document_type = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, verbose_name="Tipo de Documento")
    
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)
    
    class Meta:
        verbose_name = "Formador"
        verbose_name_plural = "Formadores"
    
    def __str__(self):
        return self.user.get_full_name() or self.user.username
    
    def is_blocked(self):
        """Check if trainer is currently blocked."""
        if self.blocked_until:
            return self.blocked_until > timezone.now().date()
        return False
    
    def can_be_assigned_to_event(self, event):
        """Check if trainer can be assigned to an event."""
        if not self.is_available or self.is_blocked():
            return False
        
        # Verificar se já está atribuído a outro evento no mesmo horário
        conflicting_events = Event.objects.filter(
            trainers=self.user,
            start_date__lt=event.end_date,
            end_date__gt=event.start_date,
            status__in=[Event.Status.APPROVED, Event.Status.PRE_AGENDA]
        ).exclude(pk=event.pk)
        
        return not conflicting_events.exists()
```

## 🌐 Integrações Externas

### 1. Google Sheets API
```python
# integrations/google/sheets_service.py
from google.oauth2 import service_account
from googleapiclient.discovery import build
from django.conf import settings
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

class GoogleSheetsService:
    """Service for interacting with Google Sheets API."""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.service = self._build_service()
    
    def _build_service(self):
        """Build authenticated Google Sheets service."""
        credentials = service_account.Credentials.from_service_account_info(
            info=settings.GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_INFO,
            scopes=['https://www.googleapis.com/auth/spreadsheets.readonly']
        )
        return build('sheets', 'v4', credentials=credentials)
    
    def get_sheet_data(self, spreadsheet_id: str, range_name: str) -> list:
        """Get data from Google Sheet."""
        try:
            result = self.service.spreadsheets().values().get(
                spreadsheetId=spreadsheet_id,
                range=range_name
            ).execute()
            
            values = result.get('values', [])
            return values
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error getting sheet data: {e}")
            raise
    
    def import_events_from_sheet(self, spreadsheet_id: str, sheet_name: str) -> list:
        """Import events from Google Sheet."""
        range_name = f"{sheet_name}!A:Z"  # Get all columns
        data = self.get_sheet_data(spreadsheet_id, range_name)
        
        if not data or len(data) < 2:
            return []
        
        headers = data[0]
        events_data = []
        
        for row in data[1:]:
            if not row or len(row) < len(headers):
                continue
                
            event_dict = dict(zip(headers, row))
            events_data.append(event_dict)
        
        return events_data
```

### 2. Google Calendar API
```python
# integrations/google/calendar_service.py
from google.oauth2 import service_account
from googleapiclient.discovery import build
from django.conf import settings
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
import logging

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

class GoogleCalendarService:
    """Service for interacting with Google Calendar API."""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.service = self._build_service()
        self.calendar_id = settings.GOOGLE_CALENDAR_ID
    
    def _build_service(self):
        """Build authenticated Google Calendar service."""
        credentials = service_account.Credentials.from_service_account_info(
            info=settings.GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_INFO,
            scopes=['https://www.googleapis.com/auth/calendar']
        )
        return build('calendar', 'v3', credentials=credentials)
    
    def create_event(self, event_data: dict) -> str:
        """Create event in Google Calendar."""
        try:
            calendar_event = {
                'summary': event_data['title'],
                'description': event_data.get('description', ''),
                'start': {
                    'dateTime': event_data['start_date'].isoformat(),
                    'timeZone': 'America/Sao_Paulo'
                },
                'end': {
                    'dateTime': event_data['end_date'].isoformat(),
                    'timeZone': 'America/Sao_Paulo'
                },
                'location': event_data.get('location', ''),
                'status': 'confirmed'
            }
            
            if event_data.get('is_online'):
                calendar_event['location'] = 'Online'
            
            result = self.service.events().insert(
                calendarId=self.calendar_id,
                body=calendar_event
            ).execute()
            
            return result['id']
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error creating calendar event: {e}")
            raise
    
    def update_event(self, calendar_event_id: str, event_data: dict) -> None:
        """Update event in Google Calendar."""
        try:
            calendar_event = {
                'summary': event_data['title'],
                'description': event_data.get('description', ''),
                'start': {
                    'dateTime': event_data['start_date'].isoformat(),
                    'timeZone': 'America/Sao_Paulo'
                },
                'end': {
                    'dateTime': event_data['end_date'].isoformat(),
                    'timeZone': 'America/Sao_Paulo'
                },
                'location': event_data.get('location', ''),
            }
            
            self.service.events().update(
                calendarId=self.calendar_id,
                eventId=calendar_event_id,
                body=calendar_event
            ).execute()
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error updating calendar event: {e}")
            raise
    
    def delete_event(self, calendar_event_id: str) -> None:
        """Delete event from Google Calendar."""
        try:
            self.service.events().delete(
                calendarId=self.calendar_id,
                eventId=calendar_event_id
            ).execute()
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error deleting calendar event: {e}")
            raise
```

## 🔐 Segurança

### Princípios de Segurança Implementados
1. **Validação de Entrada**: Todos os dados são validados antes do processamento
2. **Permissões Granulares**: Sistema de permissões detalhado por papel e objeto
3. **Autenticação Segura**: Configuração segura de sessões e autenticação
4. **Proteção CSRF**: Token CSRF em todos os formulários
5. **SQL Injection Prevention**: Uso exclusivo do Django ORM
6. **XSS Prevention**: Escapamento automático de templates
7. **Auditoria**: Logs completos de ações do sistema

### Configurações de Segurança
```python
# settings/production.py
# Security settings
DEBUG = False
ALLOWED_HOSTS = ['aprender.com.br', 'www.aprender.com.br']

# Security headers
SECURE_CONTENT_TYPE_NOSNIFF = True
SECURE_BROWSER_XSS_FILTER = True
SECURE_SSL_REDIRECT = True
SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE = True
CSRF_COOKIE_SECURE = True
X_FRAME_OPTIONS = 'DENY'

# Session security
SESSION_COOKIE_HTTPONLY = True
SESSION_COOKIE_SAMESITE = 'Lax'
SESSION_COOKIE_AGE = 3600  # 1 hora
SESSION_SAVE_EVERY_REQUEST = True

# Password validation
AUTH_PASSWORD_VALIDATORS = [
    {
        'NAME': 'django.contrib.auth.password_validation.UserAttributeSimilarityValidator',
    },
    {
        'NAME': 'django.contrib.auth.password_validation.MinimumLengthValidator',
        'OPTIONS': {
            'min_length': 8,
        }
    },
    {
        'NAME': 'django.contrib.auth.password_validation.CommonPasswordValidator',
    },
    {
        'NAME': 'django.contrib.auth.password_validation.NumericPasswordValidator',
    },
]
```

### Exemplo de Teste Unitário
```python
# tests/unit/services/test_approval_service.py
import pytest
from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from core.models import Event, Project
from core.services import ApprovalService
from tests.factories import UserFactory, ProjectFactory, EventFactory

User = get_user_model()

class TestApprovalService:
    """Test suite for ApprovalService."""
    
    @pytest.fixture
    def approval_service(self):
        return ApprovalService()
    
    @pytest.fixture
    def approver_user(self):
        user = UserFactory()
        user.user_permissions.add(
            Permission.objects.get(codename='can_approve_event')
        )
        return user
    
    @pytest.fixture
    def pending_event(self):
        return EventFactory(status=Event.Status.PENDING)
    
    def test_approve_event_success(self, approval_service, pending_event, approver_user):
        """Test successful event approval."""
        # Act
        approved_event = approval_service.approve_event(pending_event.id, approver_user)
        
        # Assert
        assert approved_event.status == Event.Status.APPROVED
        assert approved_event.approved_by == approver_user
        assert approved_event.approved_at is not None
    
    def test_approve_event_requires_superintendency(self, approval_service):
        """Test approval logic for superintendency projects."""
        # Create a project that requires superintendency approval
        project = ProjectFactory(is_linked_to_superintendency=True)
        event = EventFactory(project=project, status=Event.Status.DRAFT)
        
        # Act
        approval_service.submit_for_approval(event)
        
        # Assert
        event.refresh_from_db()
        assert event.status == Event.Status.PENDING
    
    def test_approve_event_direct_flow(self, approval_service):
        """Test approval logic for non-superintendency projects."""
        # Create a project that doesn't require superintendency approval
        project = ProjectFactory(is_linked_to_superintendency=False)
        event = EventFactory(project=project, status=Event.Status.DRAFT)
        
        # Act
        approval_service.submit_for_approval(event)
        
        # Assert
        event.refresh_from_db()
        assert event.status == Event.Status.PRE_AGENDA
```

## 🚀 Deploy e Operações

### Configuração Docker
```dockerfile
# Dockerfile
FROM python:3.13-slim

# Set environment variables
ENV PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE 1
ENV PYTHONUNBUFFERED 1

# Set work directory
WORKDIR /app

# Install system dependencies
RUN apt-get update \
    && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \
        postgresql-client \
        build-essential \
        libpq-dev \
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

# Install Python dependencies
COPY requirements/production.txt /app/
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir -r production.txt

# Copy project
COPY . /app/

# Create static directory
RUN mkdir -p /app/staticfiles

# Collect static files
RUN python manage.py collectstatic --noinput --settings=aprender.settings.production

# Create non-root user
RUN adduser --disabled-password --gecos '' appuser
RUN chown -R appuser:appuser /app
USER appuser

# Expose port
EXPOSE 8000

# Run the application
CMD ["gunicorn", "--bind", "0.0.0.0:8000", "aprender.wsgi:application"]
```

### Docker Compose
```yaml
# docker-compose.yml
version: '3.8'

services:
  db:
    image: postgres:15
    environment:
      POSTGRES_DB: aprender_db
      POSTGRES_USER: aprender_user
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: ${DB_PASSWORD}
    volumes:
      - postgres_data:/var/lib/postgresql/data
    ports:
      - "5432:5432"

  web:
    build: .
    command: python manage.py runserver 0.0.0.0:8000
    volumes:
      - .:/app
      - static_volume:/app/staticfiles
    ports:
      - "8000:8000"
    environment:
      - DEBUG=True
      - DB_NAME=aprender_db
      - DB_USER=aprender_user
      - DB_PASSWORD=${DB_PASSWORD}
      - DB_HOST=db
      - DB_PORT=5432
    depends_on:
      - db

  redis:
    image: redis:7-alpine
    ports:
      - "6379:6379"

volumes:
  postgres_data:
  static_volume:
```

### GitHub Actions para Deploy
```yaml
# .github/workflows/deploy.yml
name: Deploy to Production

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]

jobs:
  deploy:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      
      - name: Set up Python
        uses: actions/setup-python@v4
        with:
          python-version: '3.13'
      
      - name: Install dependencies
        run: |
          python -m pip install --upgrade pip
          pip install -r requirements/production.txt
      
      - name: Run tests
        run: |
          python manage.py test --settings=aprender.settings.testing
      
      - name: Build and push Docker image
        run: |
          echo ${{ secrets.DOCKER_PASSWORD }} | docker login -u ${{ secrets.DOCKER_USERNAME }} --password-stdin
          docker build -t aprender/app:latest .
          docker push aprender/app:latest
      
      - name: Deploy to Railway
        run: |
          railway login --token ${{ secrets.RAILWAY_TOKEN }}
          railway up
```

## 🤖 Passo 2: Implementar o Servidor MCP

### 📍 Onde: `aprender/neural_system/` (nova pasta)

### 📝 O Que Criar: Estrutura completa do MCP

#### 1. Criar a pasta neural_system
```bash
# Comando para criar
mkdir -p aprender/neural_system
```

#### 2. Criar `mcp_server_aprender.py`
```bash
# Comando para criar
touch aprender/neural_system/mcp_server_aprender.py
```

**Conteúdo Completo:**
```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Servidor MCP para fornecer contexto do Sistema APRENDER
"""

import asyncio
import json
import logging
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional
from mcp.server import Server
from mcp.server.stdio import stdio_server

# Configuração de logging
logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# Inicialização do servidor MCP
server = Server("aprender-context")

# Diretório base da documentação
DOCS_DIR = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent  # Volta 3 níveis para a raiz

@server.list_tools()
async def list_tools() -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Lista ferramentas disponíveis no servidor MCP"""
    return [
        {
            "name": "get_architecture_patterns",
            "description": "Retorna padrões de arquitetura do sistema",
            "inputSchema": {
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {},
            },
        },
        {
            "name": "get_python_patterns",
            "description": "Retorna padrões de código Python do sistema",
            "inputSchema": {
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {},
            },
        },
        {
            "name": "get_security_guidelines",
            "description": "Retorna diretrizes de segurança do sistema",
            "inputSchema": {
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {},
            },
        },
        {
            "name": "get_business_rules",
            "description": "Retorna regras de negócio específicas",
            "inputSchema": {
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {
                    "rule_category": {
                        "type": "string",
                        "description": "Categoria da regra (approval, permissions, hierarchy)"
                    }
                },
            },
        },
        {
            "name": "validate_code_pattern",
            "description": "Valida se código segue padrões do sistema",
            "inputSchema": {
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {
                    "code": {
                        "type": "string",
                        "description": "Código Python para validar"
                    },
                    "context": {
                        "type": "string",
                        "description": "Contexto do código (view, model, service, api)"
                    }
                },
            },
        },
        {
            "name": "check_security_vulnerabilities",
            "description": "Verifica vulnerabilidades de segurança no código",
            "inputSchema": {
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {
                    "code": {
                        "type": "string",
                        "description": "Código Python para analisar"
                    }
                },
            }
        },
        {
            "name": "get_system_context",
            "description": "Retorna contexto completo do sistema",
            "inputSchema": {
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {},
            },
        }
    ]

def _read_documentation_file(filename: str) -> Optional[str]:
    """Lê um arquivo de documentação."""
    file_path = DOCS_DIR / filename
    if file_path.exists():
        try:
            with open(file_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                return f.read()
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Erro ao ler arquivo {filename}: {e}")
    return None

@server.call_tool()
async def call_tool(name: str, arguments: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Executa as ferramentas do servidor MCP"""
    
    try:
        if name == "get_architecture_patterns":
            content = _read_documentation_file("ARQUITETURA_REFERENCIA.md")
            if content:
                return [{"type": "text", "text": content}]
            return [{"type": "text", "text": "Arquivo de arquitetura não encontrado"}]
        
        elif name == "get_python_patterns":
            content = _read_documentation_file("PADROES_CODIGO_PYTHON.md")
            if content:
                return [{"type": "text", "text": content}]
            return [{"type": "text", "text": "Arquivo de padrões Python não encontrado"}]
        
        elif name == "get_security_guidelines":
            content = _read_documentation_file("GUIA_SEGURANCA.md")
            if content:
                return [{"type": "text", "text": content}]
            return [{"type": "text", "text": "Arquivo de segurança não encontrado"}]
        
        elif name == "get_business_rules":
            category = arguments.get("rule_category", "all")
            
            # Buscar regras específicas na documentação
            context_content = _read_documentation_file("CLAUDE_CONTEXT_PACKAGE.md")
            if context_content:
                # Extrair seção relevante baseado na categoria
                if category == "approval":
                    # Extrair seção de aprovação
                    lines = context_content.split('\n')
                    approval_section = []
                    in_section = False
                    
                    for line in lines:
                        if "Sistema de Aprovação de Eventos" in line:
                            in_section = True
                        elif in_section and line.startswith("## "):
                            break
                        elif in_section:
                            approval_section.append(line)
                    
                    if approval_section:
                        return [{"type": "text", "text": "\n".join(approval_section)}]
                
                elif category == "permissions":
                    # Extrair seção de permissões
                    lines = context_content.split('\n')
                    permissions_section = []
                    in_section = False
                    
                    for line in lines:
                        if "Sistema de Permissões" in line:
                            in_section = True
                        elif in_section and line.startswith("## "):
                            break
                        elif in_section:
                            permissions_section.append(line)
                    
                    if permissions_section:
                        return [{"type": "text", "text": "\n".join(permissions_section)}]
                
                elif category == "hierarchy":
                    # Extrair seção de hierarquia
                    lines = context_content.split('\n')
                    hierarchy_section = []
                    in_section = False
                    
                    for line in lines:
                        if "Hierarquia Organizacional" in line:
                            in_section = True
                        elif in_section and line.startswith("## "):
                            break
                        elif in_section:
                            hierarchy_section.append(line)
                    
                    if hierarchy_section:
                        return [{"type": "text", "text": "\n".join(hierarchy_section)}]
            
            return [{"type": "text", "text": f"Regras para categoria '{category}' não encontradas"}]
        
        elif name == "validate_code_pattern":
            code = arguments.get("code", "")
            context = arguments.get("context", "")
            
            validation_results = []
            
            # Verificações básicas de padrão
            if "def " in code:
                # Verificar type hints
                if "->" not in code and ":" not in code.split("def ")[1].split("(")[0]:
                    validation_results.append("⚠️ AVISO: Função sem type hints")
                
                # Verificar docstring
                if '"""' not in code and "'''" not in code:
                    validation_results.append("⚠️ AVISO: Função sem docstring")
            
            # Verificar uso de logging em vez de print
            if "print(" in code:
                validation_results.append("❌ ERRO: Usar logging em vez de print")
            
            # Verificar tratamento de exceções
            if "except:" in code and "except Exception" not in code:
                validation_results.append("⚠️ AVISO: Bloco except muito genérico")
            
            # Verificações específicas por contexto
            if context == "view":
                if "request.POST" in code or "request.GET" in code:
                    if "clean(" not in code and "validate(" not in code:
                        validation_results.append("❌ ERRO: Dados de entrada não validados")
                
                if not any(perm in code for perm in ["@login_required", "@permission_required", "has_perm"]):
                    validation_results.append("❌ ERRO: View sem verificação de permissão")
            
            elif context == "model":
                if "models.Model" not in code:
                    validation_results.append("❌ ERRO: Model não herda de models.Model")
                
                if "class Meta:" not in code:
                    validation_results.append("⚠️ AVISO: Model sem classe Meta")
            
            elif context == "api":
                if "permission_classes" not in code:
                    validation_results.append("❌ ERRO: API sem classes de permissão")
                
                if "serializer_class" not in code:
                    validation_results.append("❌ ERRO: API sem serializer definido")
            
            result = "\n".join(validation_results) if validation_results else "✅ Código segue os padrões do sistema"
            return [{"type": "text", "text": result}]
        
        elif name == "check_security_vulnerabilities":
            code = arguments.get("code", "")
            
            vulnerabilities = []
            
            # Verificar SQL injection potencial
            if "execute(" in code and ("%" in code or ".format(" in code or "f\"" in code):
                vulnerabilities.append("🔴 RISCO: Possível SQL injection - usar parâmetros nomeados")
            
            # Verificar hardcoded secrets
            if any(secret in code.lower() for secret in ["password", "secret", "key", "token"]):
                if "=" in code and ("'" in code or '"' in code):
                    vulnerabilities.append("🔴 RISCO: Possível segredo hardcoded")
            
            # Verificar validação de entrada
            if "request.POST" in code or "request.GET" in code:
                if "clean(" not in code and "validate(" not in code:
                    vulnerabilities.append("🔴 RISCO: Dados de entrada não validados")
            
            # Verificar XSS
            if "HttpResponse" in code and "mark_safe" not in code:
                if any(method in code for method in [".format(", "f\"", "%"]):
                    vulnerabilities.append("🟡 RISCO: Possível XSS - usar escapamento automático")
            
            # Verificar permissões
            if "def delete(" in code or "def update(" in code:
                if "has_perm" not in code and "permission_required" not in code:
                    vulnerabilities.append("🔴 RISCO: Operação crítica sem verificação de permissão")
            
            result = "\n".join(vulnerabilities) if vulnerabilities else "✅ Nenhuma vulnerabilidade óbvia detectada"
            return [{"type": "text", "text": result}]
        
        elif name == "get_system_context":
            context_content = _read_documentation_file("CLAUDE_CONTEXT_PACKAGE.md")
            if context_content:
                return [{"type": "text", "text": context_content}]
            return [{"type": "text", "text": "Contexto do sistema não encontrado"}]
        
        else:
            return [{"type": "text", "text": f"Ferramenta desconhecida: {name}"}]
    
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Erro ao executar ferramenta {name}: {e}")
        return [{"type": "text", "text": f"Erro ao executar ferramenta: {str(e)}"}]

async def main():
    """Função principal do servidor MCP"""
    async with stdio_server() as (read_stream, write_stream):
        await server.run(read_stream, write_stream)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(main())
```

#### 4. Criar `upload_to_claude.py`
```bash
# Comando para criar
touch aprender/neural_system/upload_to_claude.py
```

**Conteúdo:**
```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Script para upload de documentação do Sistema APRENDER para o Claude
"""

import os
import base64
from pathlib import Path
import anthropic
from typing import List

# Configuração da API do Claude
# NOTA: Você precisa configurar sua API key como variável de ambiente
API_KEY = os.getenv('CLAUDE_API_KEY')
if not API_KEY:
    raise ValueError("CLAUDE_API_KEY environment variable not set")

client = anthropic.Anthropic(api_key=API_KEY)

# Diretório base da documentação
DOCS_DIR = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent

# Lista de documentos essenciais para upload
ESSENTIAL_DOCS = [
    "ARQUITETURA_REFERENCIA.md",
    "PADROES_CODIGO_PYTHON.md",
    "GUIA_SEGURANCA.md",
    "CLAUDE_CONTEXT_PACKAGE.md",
    "docs/fluxo_aprovacao.md",
    "docs/hierarquia_organizacional.md",
    "docs/GRUPOS_ORGANIZACIONAIS.md",
    "docs/ANALISE_EVENTOS_MULTIPLOS_PEDAGOGICOS.md",
    "docs/REFINAMENTOS_MELHORES_PRATICAS.md",
    "docs/ESTRATEGIA_BRANCHING_DESENVOLVIMENTO.md",
]

def upload_document_to_claude(file_path: Path) -> bool:
    """Faz upload de um documento para o Claude"""
    try:
        with open(file_path, 'rb') as f:
            content = f.read()
        
        # Criar mensagem com o documento
        response = client.messages.create(
            model="claude-3-sonnet-20240229",
            max_tokens=1000,
            messages=[
                {
                    "role": "user", 
                    "content": f"""
                    Por favor, armazene este documento como contexto para futuras interações sobre o Sistema APRENDER.
                    
                    Este documento contém informações críticas sobre:
                    - Arquitetura do sistema
                    - Padrões de código Python
                    - Diretrizes de segurança
                    - Regras de negócio
                    - Fluxos de trabalho
                    
                    Use este contexto para fornecer respostas precisas e contextualizadas sobre o Sistema APRENDER.
                    """
                }
            ],
            documents=[
                {
                    "type": "text",
                    "source": {
                        "type": "base64",
                        "media_type": "text/markdown",
                        "data": base64.b64encode(content).decode('utf-8')
                    }
                }
            ]
        )
        
        print(f"✅ Documento {file_path.name} enviado com sucesso!")
        print(f"   Resposta: {response.content[0].text}")
        return True
        
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"❌ Erro ao enviar documento {file_path.name}: {e}")
        return False

def main():
    """Função principal"""
    print("🚀 Iniciando upload de documentação para o Claude...")
    print(f"📁 Diretório de documentação: {DOCS_DIR}")
    print()
    
    successful_uploads = 0
    total_docs = len(ESSENTIAL_DOCS)
    
    for doc_name in ESSENTIAL_DOCS:
        doc_path = DOCS_DIR / doc_name
        print(f"📄 Processando: {doc_name}")
        
        if doc_path.exists():
            if upload_document_to_claude(doc_path):
                successful_uploads += 1
        else:
            print(f"⚠️ Documento não encontrado: {doc_path}")
        
        print("-" * 50)
    
    print()
    print("📊 RESUMO DO UPLOAD:")
    print(f"   Total de documentos: {total_docs}")
    print(f"   Uploads bem-sucedidos: {successful_uploads}")
    print(f"   Falhas: {total_docs - successful_uploads}")
    
    if successful_uploads == total_docs:
        print("🎉 Todos os documentos foram enviados com sucesso!")
    else:
        print("⚠️ Alguns documentos não puderam ser enviados. Verifique os logs acima.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

#### 5. Criar `update_context.py`
```bash
# Comando para criar
touch aprender/neural_system/update_context.py
```

**Conteúdo:**
```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Script para atualização contínua do contexto do Claude e MCP
"""

import os
import json
import hashlib
import subprocess
import requests
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Dict, List, Optional

# Configurações
DOCS_DIR = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent
CONTEXT_DIR = Path(__file__).parent / "context"
LAST_UPDATE_FILE = CONTEXT_DIR / "last_update.json"
CLAUDE_API_KEY = os.getenv('CLAUDE_API_KEY')

def calculate_file_hash(file_path: Path) -> str:
    """Calcula hash SHA256 de um arquivo"""
    sha256_hash = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(file_path, "rb") as f:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(4096), b""):
            sha256_hash.update(chunk)
    return sha256_hash.hexdigest()

def get_file_hashes() -> Dict[str, str]:
    """Retorna hashes de todos os arquivos de documentação"""
    file_hashes = {}
    
    # Arquivos principais
    main_docs = [
        "ARQUITETURA_REFERENCIA.md",
        "PADROES_CODIGO_PYTHON.md", 
        "GUIA_SEGURANCA.md",
        "CLAUDE_CONTEXT_PACKAGE.md"
    ]
    
    for doc_name in main_docs:
        file_path = DOCS_DIR / doc_name
        if file_path.exists():
            file_hashes[doc_name] = calculate_file_hash(file_path)
    
    # Arquivos da pasta docs/
    docs_dir = DOCS_DIR / "docs"
    if docs_dir.exists():
        for file_path in docs_dir.rglob("*.md"):
            relative_path = file_path.relative_to(DOCS_DIR)
            file_hashes[str(relative_path)] = calculate_file_hash(file_path)
    
    return file_hashes

def load_last_hashes() -> Dict[str, str]:
    """Carrega hashes da última atualização"""
    if LAST_UPDATE_FILE.exists():
        with open(LAST_UPDATE_FILE, 'r') as f:
            data = json.load(f)
            return data.get("hashes", {})
    return {}

def save_last_hashes(hashes: Dict[str, str]) -> None:
    """Salva hashes da atualização atual"""
    CONTEXT_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    
    data = {
        "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
        "hashes": hashes
    }
    
    with open(LAST_UPDATE_FILE, 'w') as f:
        json.dump(data, f, indent=2)

def restart_mcp_server() -> bool:
    """Reinicia o servidor MCP para carregar nova documentação"""
    try:
        # Parar servidor MCP existente
        subprocess.run(["pkill", "-f", "mcp_server_aprender.py"], check=False)
        
        # Iniciar novo servidor MCP
        subprocess.run([
            "python", 
            str(Path(__file__).parent / "mcp_server_aprender.py")
        ], check=True)
        
        print("✅ Servidor MCP reiniciado com sucesso!")
        return True
        
    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
        print(f"❌ Erro ao reiniciar servidor MCP: {e}")
        return False

def update_claude_context(changed_files: List[str]) -> bool:
    """Atualiza contexto do Claude com arquivos modificados"""
    if not CLAUDE_API_KEY:
        print("⚠️ CLAUDE_API_KEY não configurada. Pulando atualização do Claude.")
        return False
    
    headers = {
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
        "x-api-key": CLAUDE_API_KEY,
        "anthropic-version": "2023-06-01"
    }
    
    success_count = 0
    
    for file_path in changed_files:
        full_path = DOCS_DIR / file_path
        
        if not full_path.exists():
            print(f"⚠️ Arquivo não encontrado: {file_path}")
            continue
        
        try:
            with open(full_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                content = f.read()
            
            payload = {
                "model": "claude-3-sonnet-20240229",
                "messages": [
                    {
                        "role": "user",
                        "content": f"""
                        Atualize seu contexto com esta versão atualizada do documento {file_path} do Sistema APRENDER.
                        
                        Este documento contém informações críticas que podem ter sido modificadas:
                        - Atualizações de arquitetura
                        - Novos padrões de código
                        - Mudanças nas diretrizes de segurança
                        - Atualizações de regras de negócio
                        
                        Substitua qualquer informação anterior sobre este documento com esta nova versão.
                        """
                    }
                ],
                "documents": [
                    {
                        "type": "text",
                        "source": {
                            "type": "text",
                            "content": content
                        }
                    }
                ]
            }
            
            response = requests.post(
                "https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages",
                headers=headers,
                json=payload
            )
            response.raise_for_status()
            
            print(f"✅ Contexto do Claude atualizado para {file_path}")
            success_count += 1
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"❌ Erro ao atualizar contexto do Claude para {file_path}: {e}")
    
    return success_count == len(changed_files)

def main():
    """Função principal"""
    print("🔄 Verificando atualizações na documentação...")
    print(f"📁 Diretório de documentação: {DOCS_DIR}")
    print()
    
    current_hashes = get_file_hashes()
    last_hashes = load_last_hashes()
    
    changed_files = []
    
    for file_path, current_hash in current_hashes.items():
        if file_path not in last_hashes or last_hashes[file_path] != current_hash:
            changed_files.append(file_path)
            print(f"📄 Arquivo modificado: {file_path}")
    
    if not changed_files:
        print("✅ Nenhuma atualização necessária.")
        return
    
    print(f"\n🔄 Encontrados {len(changed_files)} arquivos modificados. Atualizando contextos...")
    print("-" * 50)
    
    # Atualizar contexto do Claude
    if CLAUDE_API_KEY:
        print("🤖 Atualizando contexto do Claude...")
        claude_success = update_claude_context(changed_files)
        print(f"   Resultado: {'✅ Sucesso' if claude_success else '❌ Falha'}")
    else:
        print("⚠️ Pulando atualização do Claude (API key não configurada)")
    
    print()
    
    # Reiniciar servidor MCP
    print("🔄 Reiniciando servidor MCP...")
    mcp_success = restart_mcp_server()
    print(f"   Resultado: {'✅ Sucesso' if mcp_success else '❌ Falha'}")
    
    print()
    
    # Salvar hashes atuais
    save_last_hashes(current_hashes)
    
    print("📊 RESUMO DA ATUALIZAÇÃO:")
    print(f"   Arquivos modificados: {len(changed_files)}")
    print(f"   Atualização Claude: {'✅' if CLAUDE_API_KEY and claude_success else '⚠️'}")
    print(f"   Reinício MCP: {'✅' if mcp_success else '❌'}")
    
    if (not CLAUDE_API_KEY or claude_success) and mcp_success:
        print("🎉 Atualização de contexto concluída com sucesso!")
    else:
        print("⚠️ Alguns componentes não puderam ser atualizados. Verifique os logs acima.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

## 🔄 Passo 4: Configurar GitHub Actions

### 📍 Onde: `aprender/.github/workflows/` (pasta existente)

### 📝 O Que Criar/Atualizar: Workflows de CI/CD

#### 1. Criar `quality-check.yml`
```bash
# Comando para criar
touch aprender/.github/workflows/quality-check.yml
```

**Conteúdo:**
```yaml
name: Quality Checks

on:
  push:
    branches: [ main, develop, feature/* ]
  pull_request:
    branches: [ main, develop ]

jobs:
  quality-check:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    strategy:
      matrix:
        python-version: [3.13]

    steps:
    - name: Checkout code
      uses: actions/checkout@v4
    
    - name: Set up Python ${{ matrix.python-version }}
      uses: actions/setup-python@v4
      with:
        python-version: ${{ matrix.python-version }}
    
    - name: Cache pip dependencies
      uses: actions/cache@v3
      with:
        path: ~/.cache/pip
        key: ${{ runner.os }}-pip-${{ hashFiles('**/requirements*.txt') }}
        restore-keys: |
          ${{ runner.os }}-pip-
    
    - name: Install dependencies
      run: |
        python -m pip install --upgrade pip
        pip install -r requirements/dev.txt
    
    - name: Run Black formatting check
      run: black --check --diff .
      continue-on-error: true
    
    - name: Run isort import sorting check
      run: isort --check-only --diff .
      continue-on-error: true
    
    - name: Run Flake8 style check
      run: flake8 . --statistics
      continue-on-error: true
    
    - name: Run MyPy type checking
      run: mypy --ignore-missing-imports .
      continue-on-error: true
    
    - name: Run Bandit security check
      run: bandit -r . -f json -o bandit-report.json
      continue-on-error: true
    
    - name: Upload Bandit report
      uses: actions/upload-artifact@v3
      if: always()
      with:
        name: bandit-report-${{ matrix.python-version }}
        path: bandit-report.json
    
    - name: Run safety check for dependencies
      run: safety check --json --output safety-report.json
      continue-on-error: true
    
    - name: Upload Safety report
      uses: actions/upload-artifact@v3
      if: always()
      with:
        name: safety-report-${{ matrix.python-version }}
        path: safety-report.json
    
    - name: Run tests with coverage
      run: |
        pytest --cov=. --cov-report=xml --cov-report=term-missing --cov-report=html
      continue-on-error: true
    
    - name: Upload coverage to Codecov
      uses: codecov/codecov-action@v3
      if: matrix.python-version == '3.13'
      with:
        file: ./coverage.xml
        flags: unittests
        name: codecov-umbrella
        fail_ci_if_error: false
    
    - name: Upload coverage reports
      uses: actions/upload-artifact@v3
      if: always()
      with:
        name: coverage-report-${{ matrix.python-version }}
        path: |
          coverage.xml
          htmlcov/

  security-scan:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    needs: quality-check
    if: github.event_name == 'pull_request'
    
    steps:
    - name: Checkout code
      uses: actions/checkout@v4
    
    - name: Run Trivy vulnerability scanner
      uses: aquasecurity/trivy-action@master
      with:
        scan-type: 'fs'
        scan-ref: '.'
        format: 'sarif'
        output: 'trivy-results.sarif'
    
    - name: Upload Trivy scan results to GitHub Security tab
      uses: github/codeql-action/upload-sarif@v2
      with:
        sarif_file: 'trivy-results.sarif'
```

#### 2. Atualizar `deploy.yml` (se existir)
```bash
# Editar arquivo existente
vim aprender/.github/workflows/deploy.yml
```

**Adicionar estas etapas antes do deploy:**

    - name: Run quality checks
      run: |
        pip install -r requirements/dev.txt

#### 2. Criar `test_mcp_server.py`

**Conteúdo:**
```python
import pytest
import asyncio
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import Mock, patch, AsyncMock
import sys
import os

# Adicionar o diretório neural_system ao path
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent / "neural_system"))

from mcp_server_aprender import server, _read_documentation_file

class TestMCPServer:
    """Test suite for MCP server functionality."""
    
    def test_list_tools(self):
        """Test that tools are listed correctly."""
        tools = asyncio.run(server.list_tools())
        
        assert isinstance(tools, list)
        assert len(tools) > 0
        
        # Check that essential tools are present
        tool_names = [tool["name"] for tool in tools]
        essential_tools = [
            "get_architecture_patterns",
            "get_python_patterns", 
            "get_security_guidelines",
            "get_business_rules",
            "validate_code_pattern",
            "check_security_vulnerabilities",
            "get_system_context"
        ]
        
        for tool in essential_tools:
            assert tool in tool_names, f"Tool {tool} not found"
    
    def test_read_documentation_file_success(self):
        """Test successful reading of documentation files."""
        # Create a temporary test file
        test_content = "# Test Document\nThis is a test."
        test_file = Path("/tmp/test_doc.md")
        
        try:
            test_file.write_text(test_content)
            
            # Test reading the file
            content = _read_documentation_file(str(test_file))
            assert content == test_content
        finally:
            if test_file.exists():
                test_file.unlink()
    
    def test_read_documentation_file_not_found(self):
        """Test reading non-existent documentation file."""
        content = _read_documentation_file("/nonexistent/file.md")
        assert content is None
    
    @pytest.mark.asyncio
    async def test_get_architecture_patterns(self):
        """Test getting architecture patterns."""
        # Mock the documentation file
        mock_content = "# Architecture Patterns\nTest content"
        
        with patch('mcp_server_aprender._read_documentation_file', return_value=mock_content):
            result = await server.call_tool("get_architecture_patterns", {})
            
            assert len(result) == 1
            assert result[0]["type"] == "text"
            assert result[0]["text"] == mock_content
    
    @pytest.mark.asyncio
    async def test_validate_code_pattern_good_code(self):
        """Test code validation with good code."""
        good_code = '''
def test_function(user_id: int) -> str:
    """Test function with proper typing and docstring."""
    return f"User {user_id}"
'''
        
        result = await server.call_tool("validate_code_pattern", {
            "code": good_code,
            "context": "view"
        })
        
        assert len(result) == 1
        assert result[0]["type"] == "text"
        assert "✅ Código segue os padrões do sistema" in result[0]["text"]
    
    @pytest.mark.asyncio
    async def test_validate_code_pattern_bad_code(self):
        """Test code validation with problematic code."""
        bad_code = '''
def bad_function(user_id):
    print(f"User {user_id}")
    return "done"
'''
        
        result = await server.call_tool("validate_code_pattern", {
            "code": bad_code,
            "context": "view"
        })
        
        assert len(result) == 1
        assert result[0]["type"] == "text"
        assert "ERRO" in result[0]["text"]
        assert "print(" in result[0]["text"]
    
    @pytest.mark.asyncio
    async def test_check_security_vulnerabilities_safe_code(self):
        """Test security check with safe code."""
        safe_code = '''
def safe_view(request, user_id: int):
    user = User.objects.get(pk=user_id)
    return render(request, 'template.html', {'user': user})
'''
        
        result = await server.call_tool("check_security_vulnerabilities", {
            "code": safe_code
        })
        
        assert len(result) == 1
        assert result[0]["type"] == "text"
        assert "✅ Nenhuma vulnerabilidade óbvia detectada" in result[0]["text"]
    
    @pytest.mark.asyncio
    async def test_check_security_vulnerabilities_vulnerable_code(self):
        """Test security check with vulnerable code."""
        vulnerable_code = '''
def vulnerable_view(request):
    user_id = request.GET['user_id']
    query = "SELECT * FROM auth_user WHERE id = %s" % user_id
    return execute_query(query)
'''
        
        result = await server.call_tool("check_security_vulnerabilities", {
            "code": vulnerable_code
        })
        
        assert len(result) == 1
        assert result[0]["type"] == "text"
        assert "RISCO" in result[0]["text"]
        assert "SQL injection" in result[0]["text"]
    
    @pytest.mark.asyncio
    async def test_get_business_rules_approval(self):
        """Test getting business rules for approval."""
        mock_context = """
# Sistema de Aprovação de Eventos

O sistema possui um fluxo de aprovação diferenciado baseado no tipo de projeto.

## Projetos Vinculados à Superintendência
Lista: AMMA, CATAVENTOS, CIRANDAR
Fluxo: Coordenador → PENDENTE → Superintendência → PRE_AGENDA → Controle → APROVADO
"""
        
        with patch('mcp_server_aprender._read_documentation_file', return_value=mock_context):
            result = await server.call_tool("get_business_rules", {
                "rule_category": "approval"
            })
            
            assert len(result) == 1
            assert result[0]["type"] == "text"
            assert "Sistema de Aprovação de Eventos" in result[0]["text"]
            assert "Projetos Vinculados à Superintendência" in result[0]["text"]
    
    @pytest.mark.asyncio
    async def test_unknown_tool(self):
        """Test handling of unknown tools."""
        result = await server.call_tool("unknown_tool", {})
        
        assert len(result) == 1
        assert result[0]["type"] == "text"
        assert "Ferramenta desconhecida" in result[0]["text"]
```

#### 3. Criar `test_context_updates.py`
```bash
# Comando para criar
touch aprender/tests/neural_system/test_context_updates.py
```

**Conteúdo:**
```python
import pytest
import json
import hashlib
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import Mock, patch, MagicMock
import sys
import os

# Adicionar o diretório neural_system ao path
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent / "neural_system"))

from update_context import (
    calculate_file_hash,
    get_file_hashes,
    load_last_hashes,
    save_last_hashes,
    update_claude_context,
    restart_mcp_server
)

class TestContextUpdates:
    """Test suite for context update functionality."""
    
    def test_calculate_file_hash(self):
        """Test file hash calculation."""
        # Create a temporary test file
        test_content = "Test content for hash calculation"
        test_file = Path("/tmp/test_hash.txt")
        
        try:
            test_file.write_text(test_content)
            hash1 = calculate_file_hash(test_file)
            hash2 = calculate_file_hash(test_file)
            
            # Hash should be consistent
            assert hash1 == hash2
            assert len(hash1) == 64  # SHA256 produces 64 character hex string
            
            # Different content should produce different hash
            test_file.write_text("Different content")
            hash3 = calculate_file_hash(test_file)
            assert hash1 != hash3
            
        finally:
            if test_file.exists():
                test_file.unlink()
    
    def test_get_file_hashes(self):
        """Test getting hashes of documentation files."""
        # Create temporary documentation structure
        docs_dir = Path("/tmp/test_docs")
        docs_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        
        try:
            # Create test files
            (docs_dir / "ARQUITETURA_REFERENCIA.md").write_text("# Architecture")
            (docs_dir / "PADROES_CODIGO_PYTHON.md").write_text("# Patterns")
            
            # Create docs subdirectory
            docs_subdir = docs_dir / "docs"
            docs_subdir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
            (docs_subdir / "fluxo_aprovacao.md").write_text("# Approval Flow")
            
            # Mock DOCS_DIR
            with patch('update_context.DOCS_DIR', docs_dir):
                hashes = get_file_hashes()
                
                # Should contain all documentation files
                assert "ARQUITETURA_REFERENCIA.md" in hashes
                assert "PADROES_CODIGO_PYTHON.md" in hashes
                assert "docs/fluxo_aprovacao.md" in hashes
                assert len(hashes) == 3
                
        finally:
            # Cleanup
            import shutil
            if docs_dir.exists():
                shutil.rmtree(docs_dir)
    
    def test_load_last_hashes_no_file(self):
        """Test loading last hashes when no file exists."""
        with patch('update_context.LAST_UPDATE_FILE', Path("/nonexistent/file.json")):
            hashes = load_last_hashes()
            assert hashes == {}
    
    def test_save_and_load_last_hashes(self):
        """Test saving and loading last hashes."""
        # Create temporary context directory
        context_dir = Path("/tmp/test_context")
        context_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        
        try:
            with patch('update_context.CONTEXT_DIR', context_dir):
                test_hashes = {
                    "file1.md": "hash1",
                    "file2.md": "hash2"
                }
                
                save_last_hashes(test_hashes)
                
                # Verify file was created
                last_update_file = context_dir / "last_update.json"
                assert last_update_file.exists()
                
                # Load and verify content
                loaded_hashes = load_last_hashes()
                assert loaded_hashes == test_hashes
                
                # Verify file structure
                with open(last_update_file, 'r') as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                    assert "timestamp" in data
                    assert "hashes" in data
                    assert data["hashes"] == test_hashes
                    
        finally:
            import shutil
            if context_dir.exists():
                shutil.rmtree(context_dir)
    
    @patch('update_context.requests.post')
    def test_update_claude_context_success(self, mock_post):
        """Test successful Claude context update."""
        # Mock successful API response
        mock_response = Mock()
        mock_response.raise_for_status.return_value = None
        mock_post.return_value = mock_response
        
        with patch('update_context.CLAUDE_API_KEY', 'test_key'):
            result = update_claude_context(['test_file.md'])
            assert result is True
            
            # Verify API was called
            mock_post.assert_called_once()
            
            # Verify request structure
            call_args = mock_post.call_args
            assert 'headers' in call_args[1]
            assert 'json' in call_args[1]
            assert call_args[1]['json']['model'] == 'claude-3-sonnet-20240229'
    
    def test_update_claude_context_no_api_key(self):
        """Test Claude context update without API key."""
        with patch('update_context.CLAUDE_API_KEY', None):
            result = update_claude_context(['test_file.md'])
            assert result is False
    
    @patch('update_context.subprocess.run')
    def test_restart_mcp_server_success(self, mock_run):
        """Test successful MCP server restart."""
        mock_run.return_value = None
        
        result = restart_mcp_server()
        assert result is True
        
        # Verify subprocess was called twice (pkill and python)
        assert mock_run.call_count == 2
    
    @patch('update_context.subprocess.run')
    def test_restart_mcp_server_failure(self, mock_run):
        """Test MCP server restart failure."""
        mock_run.side_effect = Exception("Command failed")
        
        result = restart_mcp_server()
        assert result is False
```

# Criar script de teste
cat > test_mcp.py << 'EOF'
import asyncio
from mcp.client.session import ClientSession
from mcp.client.stdio import stdio_client

async def test_mcp():
    async with stdio_client() as (read, write):
        async with ClientSession(read, write) as session:
            # Initialize
            await session.initialize()
            
            # List tools
            tools = await session.list_tools()
            print("Available tools:", [tool.name for tool in tools.tools])
            
            # Test architecture patterns
            result = await session.call_tool("get_architecture_patterns", {})
            print("Architecture patterns length:", len(result.content[0].text))
            
            # Test code validation
            test_code = '''
def bad_function(user_id):
    print(f"User {user_id}")
    return "done"
'''
            result = await session.call_tool("validate_code_pattern", {
                "code": test_code,
                "context": "view"
            })
            print("Code validation result:", result.content[0].text)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(test_mcp())
EOF

# Executar teste
python test_mcp.py
```

> O fato de o Sistema APRENDER estar centralizado em Docker faz uma diferença significativa em vários aspectos do nosso plano de implementação do sistema neural. Vamos considerar os principais impactos:
> 
> 1. **Estrutura de Arquivos**: Os arquivos precisam ser criados nos locais corretos dentro dos containers Docker
> 2. **Caminhos e Acessibilidade**: Os caminhos absolutos serão diferentes dentro dos containers
> 3. **Persistência de Dados**: Precisamos garantir que os arquivos de documentação e configurações sejam persistentes
> 4. **Comunicação entre Containers**: O servidor MCP precisará ser acessível tanto para o Cursor (fora do container) quanto para o Claude
> 5. **Variáveis de Ambiente**: A configuração de variáveis de ambiente será feita através do Docker
> 6. **Build e Deploy**: O processo de build precisa incluir todos os novos arquivos e dependências
> 
> Vamos ajustar o plano considerando a arquitetura Docker existente.

# 🐳 Impacto do Docker no Sistema Neural - Análise Completa

Excelente informação! O fato de o Sistema APRENDER estar **centralizado em Docker** faz uma **diferença significativa** em todas as instalações e criações de arquivos. Vamos analisar os impactos e ajustar o plano.

---

## 🔄 Como o Docker Muda Tudo

### 📋 **Impactos Principais:**

1. **Estrutura de Arquivos**: Arquivos precisam ser persistidos e acessíveis
2. **Caminhos**: Os caminhos dentro dos containers são diferentes do host
3. **Comunicação**: O servidor MCP precisa ser acessível externamente
4. **Variáveis de Ambiente**: Configuração via Docker em vez de arquivos .env
5. **Dependências**: Precisam ser incluídas na imagem Docker
6. **Persistência**: Volumes para documentação e configurações

---

## 🔧 Ajustes Necessários

### 1. **Dockerfile Atualizado**

```dockerfile
# Dockerfile atualizado para incluir o sistema neural
FROM python:3.13-slim

# Set environment variables
ENV PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE 1
ENV PYTHONUNBUFFERED 1
ENV DOCKERIZED=true

# Set work directory
WORKDIR /app

# Install system dependencies
RUN apt-get update \
    && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \
        postgresql-client \
        build-essential \
        libpq-dev \
        curl \
        git \
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

# Create app user
RUN adduser --disabled-password --gecos '' appuser

# Copy requirements first for better layer caching
COPY requirements/ /app/requirements/
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir -r /app/requirements/production.txt
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir -r /app/requirements/dev.txt

# Copy application code
COPY --chown=appuser:appuser . /app/

# Create directories for neural system
RUN mkdir -p /app/neural_system /app/docker-data/docs /app/docker-data/logs /app/docker-data/context
RUN chown -R appuser:appuser /app/neural_system /app/docker-data

# Install MCP dependencies
COPY --chown=appuser:appuser neural_system/requirements_mcp.txt /app/neural_system/
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir -r /app/neural_system/requirements_mcp.txt

# Copy neural system files
COPY --chown=appuser:appuser neural_system/ /app/neural_system/

# Copy documentation files (serão montados via volume)
# COPY docs/ /app/docker-data/docs/

# Copy entrypoint script
COPY docker-entrypoint.sh /entrypoint.sh
RUN chmod +x /entrypoint.sh

# Create static directory
RUN mkdir -p /app/staticfiles && chown appuser:appuser /app/staticfiles

# Switch to non-root user
USER appuser

# Expose port
EXPOSE 8000

# Health check
HEALTHCHECK --interval=30s --timeout=10s --start-period=5s --retries=3 \
    CMD curl -f http://localhost:8000/health/ || exit 1

# Set entrypoint
ENTRYPOINT ["/entrypoint.sh"]

# Default command
CMD ["gunicorn", "--bind", "0.0.0.0:8000", "--workers", "3", "aprender.wsgi:application"]
```

### 2. **docker-compose.yml Atualizado**

```yaml
# docker-compose.yml atualizado
version: '3.8'

services:
  db:
    image: postgres:15
    environment:
      POSTGRES_DB: aprender_db
      POSTGRES_USER: aprender_user
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: ${DB_PASSWORD}
    volumes:
      - postgres_data:/var/lib/postgresql/data
      - ./docker-data/backups:/backups
    ports:
      - "5432:5432"
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "pg_isready -U aprender_user -d aprender_db"]
      interval: 10s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 5

  redis:
    image: redis:7-alpine
    ports:
      - "6379:6379"
    volumes:
      - redis_data:/data
    command: redis-server --appendonly yes

  web:
    build: .
    command: python manage.py runserver 0.0.0.0:8000
    volumes:
      - .:/app
      - docker-data/docs:/app/docker-data/docs
      - docker-data/logs:/app/docker-data/logs
      - docker-data/context:/app/docker-data/context
      - ./staticfiles:/app/staticfiles
    ports:
      - "8000:8000"
    environment:
      - DEBUG=${DEBUG}
      - DB_NAME=aprender_db
      - DB_USER=aprender_user
      - DB_PASSWORD=${DB_PASSWORD}
      - DB_HOST=db
      - DB_PORT=5432
      - REDIS_URL=redis://redis:6379/0
      - CLAUDE_API_KEY=${CLAUDE_API_KEY}
      - GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_INFO=${GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_INFO}
      - GOOGLE_CALENDAR_ID=${GOOGLE_CALENDAR_ID}
      - DOCS_DIR=/app/docker-data/docs
    depends_on:
      db:
        condition: service_healthy
      redis:
        condition: service_started
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD", "curl", "-f", "http://localhost:8000/health/"]
      interval: 30s
      timeout: 10s
      retries: 3
      start_period: 40s

  # Servidor MCP dedicado (opcional)
  mcp-server:
    build: .
    command: python neural_system/mcp_server_aprender.py
    volumes:
      - .:/app
      - docker-data/docs:/app/docker-data/docs
      - docker-data/context:/app/docker-data/context
    environment:
      - DOCS_DIR=/app/docker-data/docs
      - PYTHONPATH=/app
      - CLAUDE_API_KEY=${CLAUDE_API_KEY}
    ports:
      - "8001:8001"  # Porta diferente para o servidor MCP
    profiles:
      - mcp
    depends_on:
      web:
        condition: service_healthy

volumes:
  postgres_data:
  redis_data:
  docker-data:
    driver: local
```

### 3. **docker-entrypoint.sh**

```bash
#!/bin/bash
set -e

# Wait for database to be ready
echo "Waiting for database to be ready..."
until python -c "import psycopg2; psycopg2.connect(host='db', dbname='aprender_db', user='aprender_user', password='${DB_PASSWORD}')"; do
    echo "Database is unavailable - sleeping"
    sleep 1
done

echo "Database is ready!"

# Run migrations
echo "Running migrations..."
python manage.py migrate --noinput

# Collect static files
echo "Collecting static files..."
python manage.py collectstatic --noinput --clear

# Create superuser if not exists
echo "Creating superuser if not exists..."
python manage.py shell -c "
from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
User = get_user_model()
if not User.objects.filter(username='admin').exists():
    User.objects.create_superuser('admin', 'admin@example.com', 'admin123')
"

# Start MCP server in background (se configurado)
if [ "$ENABLE_MCP_SERVER" = "true" ]; then
    echo "Starting MCP server..."
    python neural_system/mcp_server_aprender.py &
fi

# Execute the command
exec "$@"
```
