#### **mcp-server/main.py**
```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Servidor MCP Premium para o Sistema APRENDER
"""

import asyncio
import json
import logging
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
import uvicorn
from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from pydantic import BaseModel
import redis
import elasticsearch
from langchain_openai import ChatOpenAI
from langchain.chains import ConversationChain
from langchain.memory import ConversationBufferMemory
from langchain.prompts import PromptTemplate

# Configuração de logging avançada
logging.basicConfig(
    level=logging.INFO,
    format='%(asctime)s - %(name)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s',
    handlers=[
        logging.FileHandler('/var/log/mcp-server.log'),
        logging.StreamHandler(),
        logging.handlers.SysLogHandler(address='/dev/log')
    ]
)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

class MCPRequest(BaseModel):
    """Modelo para requisições MCP"""
    tool: str
    parameters: Dict[str, Any]
    context: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None

class MCPResponse(BaseModel):
    """Modelo para respostas MCP"""
    success: bool
    data: Any
    error: Optional[str] = None
    metadata: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None

class PremiumMCPServer:
    """Servidor MCP Premium com recursos avançados"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.app = FastAPI(
            title="Sistema APRENDER MCP Server",
            description="Servidor MCP premium com IA integrada",
            version="2.0.0"
        )
        
        # Configuração de clientes
        self.redis_client = redis.Redis(
            host='localhost', port=6379, db=0, decode_responses=True
        )
        self.es_client = elasticsearch.Elasticsearch(['http://localhost:9200'])
        self.llm = ChatOpenAI(model="gpt-4", temperature=0.1)
        
        # Carregar conhecimento do sistema
        self.system_knowledge = self._load_system_knowledge()
        
        # Memória de conversação
        self.conversation_memory = ConversationBufferMemory()
        
        # Configurar middleware
        self._setup_middleware()
        
        # Configurar rotas
        self._setup_routes()
    
    def _load_system_knowledge(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Carrega conhecimento do sistema do arquivo JSON"""
        try:
            knowledge_path = Path("/app/neural-system/knowledge-base/system-context.json")
            with open(knowledge_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                return json.load(f)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Erro ao carregar conhecimento do sistema: {e}")
            return {}
    
    def _setup_middleware(self):
        """Configura middleware do FastAPI"""
        self.app.add_middleware(
            CORSMiddleware,
            allow_origins=["*"],
            allow_credentials=True,
            allow_methods=["*"],
            allow_headers=["*"],
        )
    
    def _setup_routes(self):
        """Configura rotas da API"""
        
        @self.app.get("/health")
        async def health_check():
            """Health check endpoint"""
            return {"status": "healthy", "version": "2.0.0"}
        
        @self.app.post("/mcp/invoke")
        async def invoke_tool(request: MCPRequest):
            """Invoca ferramenta MCP"""
            try:
                result = await self._execute_tool(request.tool, request.parameters, request.context)
                return MCPResponse(success=True, data=result)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"Erro ao executar ferramenta {request.tool}: {e}")
                return MCPResponse(success=False, error=str(e))
        
        @self.app.get("/mcp/tools")
        async def list_tools():
            """Lista ferramentas disponíveis"""
            tools = [
                {
                    "name": "get_architecture_patterns",
                    "description": "Retorna padrões de arquitetura do sistema",
                    "parameters": {}
                },
                {
                    "name": "get_business_rules",
                    "description": "Retorna regras de negócio específicas",
                    "parameters": {
                        "category": {"type": "string", "enum": ["approval", "permissions", "hierarchy"]}
                    }
                },
                {
                    "name": "validate_code",
                    "description": "Valida código usando análise estática e IA",
                    "parameters": {
                        "code": {"type": "string"},
                        "language": {"type": "string", "enum": ["python", "typescript", "javascript"]},
                        "context": {"type": "string"}
                    }
                },
                {
                    "name": "security_analysis",
                    "description": "Análise de segurança avançada",
                    "parameters": {
                        "code": {"type": "string"},
                        "scan_type": {"type": "string", "enum": ["sast", "dast", "dependency"]}
                    }
                },
                {
                    "name": "architecture_advisor",
                    "description": "Consultor de arquitetura com IA",
                    "parameters": {
                        "question": {"type": "string"},
                        "context": {"type": "object"}
                    }
                },
                {
                    "name": "documentation_search",
                    "description": "Busca semântica na documentação",
                    "parameters": {
                        "query": {"type": "string"},
                        "limit": {"type": "integer", "default": 10}
                    }
                }
            ]
            return {"tools": tools}
    
    async def _execute_tool(self, tool_name: str, parameters: Dict[str, Any], context: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Any:
        """Executa ferramenta específica com IA"""
        
        if tool_name == "get_architecture_patterns":
            return self.system_knowledge.get("architecture_patterns", {})
        
        elif tool_name == "get_business_rules":
            category = parameters.get("category", "all")
            business_rules = self.system_knowledge.get("business_rules", {})
            if category != "all":
                return business_rules.get(category, {})
            return business_rules
        
        elif tool_name == "validate_code":
            return await self._validate_code_with_ai(parameters)
        
        elif tool_name == "security_analysis":
            return await self._security_analysis_with_ai(parameters)
        
        elif tool_name == "architecture_advisor":
            return await self._architecture_advisor_with_ai(parameters)
        
        elif tool_name == "documentation_search":
            return await self._semantic_search(parameters)
        
        else:
            raise ValueError(f"Ferramenta desconhecida: {tool_name}")
    
    async def _validate_code_with_ai(self, parameters: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Validação de código avançada com IA"""
        code = parameters["code"]
        language = parameters["language"]
        context = parameters.get("context", "")
        
        # Template de prompt para validação
        validation_prompt = PromptTemplate(
            template="""
            Você é um especialista em análise de código e arquitetura de software.
            
            Analise o seguinte código {language} no contexto do Sistema APRENDER:
            
            Contexto: {context}
            
            Código:
            ```{language}
            {code}
            ```
            
            Forneça uma análise completa incluindo:
            1. Qualidade do código e aderência a padrões
            2. Potenciais bugs ou problemas
            3. Vulnerabilidades de segurança
            4. Sugestões de melhoria
            5. Compatibilidade com a arquitetura do sistema
            
            Use o conhecimento do Sistema APRENDER para referência.
            """,
            input_variables=["language", "context", "code"]
        )
        
        # Criar chain de conversação
        chain = ConversationChain(
            llm=self.llm,
            memory=ConversationBufferMemory(),
            prompt=validation_prompt
        )
        
        # Executar análise
        result = chain.invoke({
            "language": language,
            "context": context,
            "code": code
        })
        
        return {
            "analysis": result["response"],
            "metadata": {
                "language": language,
                "code_length": len(code),
                "timestamp": asyncio.get_event_loop().time()
            }
        }
    
    async def _security_analysis_with_ai(self, parameters: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Análise de segurança avançada com IA"""
        code = parameters["code"]
        scan_type = parameters["scan_type"]
        
        security_prompt = PromptTemplate(
            template="""
            Você é um especialista em segurança cibernética e análise de vulnerabilidades.
            
            Realize uma análise de segurança {scan_type} no seguinte código:
            
            Código:
            ```python
            {code}
            ```
            
            Forneça:
            1. Identificação de vulnerabilidades específicas
            2. Nível de risco (Crítico, Alto, Médio, Baixo)
            3. Recomendações específicas de mitigação
            4. Referência a frameworks de segurança (OWASP, NIST)
            
            Considere as diretrizes de segurança do Sistema APRENDER.
            """,
            input_variables=["scan_type", "code"]
        )
        
        chain = ConversationChain(
            llm=self.llm,
            memory=ConversationBufferMemory(),
            prompt=security_prompt
        )
        
        result = chain.invoke({
            "scan_type": scan_type,
            "code": code
        })
        
        return {
            "security_analysis": result["response"],
            "scan_type": scan_type,
            "timestamp": asyncio.get_event_loop().time()
        }
    
    async def _architecture_advisor_with_ai(self, parameters: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Consultor de arquitetura com IA"""
        question = parameters["question"]
        context = parameters.get("context", {})
        
        advisor_prompt = PromptTemplate(
            template="""
            Você é um arquiteto de software sênior especialista no Sistema APRENDER.
            
            Contexto do sistema:
            {context}
            
            Pergunta: {question}
            
            Baseado no conhecimento do Sistema APRENDER e melhores práticas de arquitetura,
            forneça uma resposta detalhada incluindo:
            1. Análise da questão
            2. Recomendações específicas
            3. Padrões de projeto relevantes
            4. Considerações de implementação
            5. Referências a ADRs (Architecture Decision Records)
            """,
            input_variables=["context", "question"]
        )
        
        chain = ConversationChain(
            llm=self.llm,
            memory=ConversationBufferMemory(),
            prompt=advisor_prompt
        )
        
        result = chain.invoke({
            "context": json.dumps(context),
            "question": question
        })
        
        return {
            "advice": result["response"],
            "question": question,
            "timestamp": asyncio.get_event_loop().time()
        }
    
    async def _semantic_search(self, parameters: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Busca semântica na documentação"""
        query = parameters["query"]
        limit = parameters.get("limit", 10)
        
        try:
            # Busca no Elasticsearch
            search_body = {
                "query": {
                    "multi_match": {
                        "query": query,
                        "fields": ["title", "content", "tags"],
                        "type": "best_fields"
                    }
                },
                "size": limit
            }
            
            response = self.es_client.search(
                index="aprender-documentation",
                body=search_body
            )
            
            results = []
            for hit in response["hits"]["hits"]:
                results.append({
                    "title": hit["_source"]["title"],
                    "content": hit["_source"]["content"][:200] + "...",
                    "score": hit["_score"],
                    "url": hit["_source"]["url"]
                })
            
            return {
                "query": query,
                "results": results,
                "total": response["hits"]["total"]["value"]
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Erro na busca semântica: {e}")
            return {"query": query, "results": [], "error": str(e)}

# Inicialização do servidor
mcp_server = PremiumMCPServer()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    uvicorn.run(
        mcp_server.app,
        host="0.0.0.0",
        port=8001,
        log_level="info",
        access_log=True
    )
```

### 2. **AI Agents Especializados**

#### **ai-agents/code-reviewer.py**
```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Agente de IA especializado em revisão de código
"""

import asyncio
import json
from typing import List, Dict, Any
from pathlib import Path
from langchain_openai import ChatOpenAI
from langchain.schema import HumanMessage, SystemMessage
from langchain.tools import Tool
from langchain.agents import AgentType, initialize_agent

class CodeReviewerAgent:
    """Agente especializado em revisão de código"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.llm = ChatOpenAI(model="gpt-4", temperature=0.1)
        
        # Ferramentas disponíveis
        self.tools = [
            Tool(
                name="code_analysis",
                func=self._analyze_code,
                description="Analisa qualidade e padrões do código"
            ),
            Tool(
                name="security_scan",
                func=self._security_scan,
                description="Busca vulnerabilidades de segurança"
            ),
            Tool(
                name="performance_check",
                func=self._performance_check,
                description="Verifica problemas de performance"
            ),
            Tool(
                name="standards_compliance",
                func=self._check_standards,
                description="Verifica conformidade com padrões do sistema"
            )
        ]
        
        # Inicializar agente
        self.agent = initialize_agent(
            tools=self.tools,
            llm=self.llm,
            agent=AgentType.STRUCTURED_CHAT_ZERO_SHOT_REACT_DESCRIPTION,
            verbose=True
        )
    
    async def review_code(self, code: str, language: str, context: str = "") -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Realiza revisão completa do código"""
        
        system_prompt = f"""
        Você é um especialista em revisão de código do Sistema APRENDER.
        
        Contexto do sistema:
        - Backend: FastAPI + Python 3.13
        - Frontend: Next.js + TypeScript
        - Banco de dados: PostgreSQL 16
        - Arquitetura: Microservices com CQRS
        - Padrões: Repository Pattern, Service Layer
        
        Contexto específico: {context}
        
        Realize uma revisão completa do código {language} fornecido,
        considerando os padrões e requisitos do Sistema APRENDER.
        """
        
        human_message = f"""
        Por favor, revise o seguinte código {language}:
        
        ```{language}
        {code}
        ```
        
        Forneça uma análise detalhada incluindo:
        1. Qualidade geral e aderência a padrões
        2. Potenciais bugs e problemas lógicos
        3. Vulnerabilidades de segurança
        4. Questões de performance
        5. Sugestões de melhoria específicas
        """
        
        response = await self.llm.agenerate([
            SystemMessage(content=system_prompt),
            HumanMessage(content=human_message)
        ])
        
        return {
            "review": response.content,
            "metadata": {
                "language": language,
                "code_length": len(code),
                "timestamp": asyncio.get_event_loop().time()
            }
        }
    
    def _analyze_code(self, code: str) -> str:
        """Ferramenta de análise de código"""
        # Implementar análise estática básica
        issues = []
        
        # Verificar type hints
        if "def " in code and "->" not in code:
            issues.append("Falta de type hints na função")
        
        # Verificar docstrings
        if "def " in code and '"""' not in code:
            issues.append("Falta de docstring na função")
        
        # Verificar tratamento de exceções
        if "except:" in code and "except Exception" not in code:
            issues.append("Bloco except muito genérico")
        
        return f"Issues encontrados: {', '.join(issues) if issues else 'Nenhum issue encontrado'}"
    
    def _security_scan(self, code: str) -> str:
        """Ferramenta de scan de segurança"""
        vulnerabilities = []
        
        # Verificar SQL injection
        if "execute(" in code and ("%" in code or ".format(" in code:
            vulnerabilities.append("Possível SQL injection")
        
        # Verificar XSS
        if "HttpResponse" in code and "mark_safe" not in code:
            vulnerabilities.append("Possível XSS")
        
        # Verificar hardcoded secrets
        if "password" in code.lower() and "=" in code:
            vulnerabilities.append("Possível senha hardcoded")
        
        return f"Vulnerabilidades: {', '.join(vulnerabilities) if vulnerabilities else 'Nenhuma vulnerabilidade encontrada'}"
    
    def _performance_check(self, code: str) -> str:
        """Ferramenta de verificação de performance"""
        issues = []
        
        # Verificar N+1 queries
        if "for " in code and ".objects.filter(" in code:
            issues.append("Possível problema N+1 queries")
        
        # Verificar falta de índices
        if "order_by(" in code and "index" not in code.lower():
            issues.append("Query sem índice adequado")
        
        return f"Issues de performance: {', '.join(issues) if issues else 'Nenhum issue de performance'}"
    
    def _check_standards(self, code: str) -> str:
        """Ferramenta de verificação de padrões"""
        standards_issues = []
        
        # Verificar PEP 8
        if len(code.split('\n')[0]) > 88:  # Verificar primeira linha
            standards_issues.append("Linha muito longa (violação PEP 8)")
        
        # Verificar nomenclatura
        if "def " in code and not code.split("def ")[1][0].islower():
            standards_issues.append("Nome de função não segue snake_case")
        
        return f"Issues de padrões: {', '.join(standards_issues) if standards_issues else 'Nenhum issue de padrões'}"
```

### 3. **Frontend Premium com Next.js**

#### **src/components/NeuralInterface.tsx**
```typescript
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useMCP } from '@/hooks/useMCP';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Tabs, TabsContent, TabsList, TabsTrigger } from '@/components/ui/tabs';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { CodeBlock } from '@/components/ui/code-block';
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from '@/components/ui/alert';

interface ToolResult {
  success: boolean;
  data: any;
  error?: string;
  metadata?: {
    timestamp: string;
    [key: string]: any;
  };
}

export default function NeuralInterface() {
  const [selectedTool, setSelectedTool] = useState('architecture_advisor');
  const [input, setInput] = useState('');
  const [context, setContext] = useState('');
  const [results, setResults] = useState<ToolResult[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  
  const { invokeTool, availableTools } = useMCP();

  const handleInvoke = async () => {
    setIsLoading(true);
    try {
      const result = await invokeTool(selectedTool, {
        query: input,
        context: context ? JSON.parse(context) : undefined,
      });
      
      setResults(prev => [...prev, result]);
    } catch (error) {
      setResults(prev => [...prev, {
        success: false,
        data: null,
        error: error.message,
        metadata: { timestamp: new Date().toISOString() }
      }]);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const renderResult = (result: ToolResult, index: number) => {
    if (!result.success) {
      return (
        <Alert key={index} variant="destructive">
          <AlertDescription>{result.error}</AlertDescription>
        </Alert>
      );
    }

    return (
      <Card key={index} className="mt-4">
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle className="flex items-center justify-between">
            Resultado #{index + 1}
            <Badge variant="secondary">
              {new Date(result.metadata?.timestamp).toLocaleTimeString()}
            </Badge>
          </CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          <CodeBlock
            language="json"
            code={JSON.stringify(result.data, null, 2)}
          />
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    );
  };

  return (
    <div className="container mx-auto p-6 space-y-6">
      <div className="text-center mb-8">
        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-2">Sistema Neural APRENDER</h1>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground">
          Interface neural com conhecimento sênior de arquitetura e desenvolvimento
        </p>
      </div>

      <Tabs value={selectedTool} onValueChange={setSelectedTool}>
        <TabsList className="grid w-full grid-cols-3">
          <TabsTrigger value="architecture_advisor">
            Consultor de Arquitetura
          </TabsTrigger>
          <TabsTrigger value="code_validator">
            Validador de Código
          </TabsTrigger>
          <TabsTrigger value="security_analyzer">
            Analisador de Segurança
          </TabsTrigger>
        </TabsList>

        <TabsContent value="architecture_advisor" className="space-y-4">
          <Card>
            <CardHeader>
              <CardTitle>Consultor de Arquitetura</CardTitle>
              <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                Tire dúvidas sobre arquitetura, padrões de projeto e melhores práticas
              </p>
            </CardHeader>
            <CardContent className="space-y-4">
              <div>
                <label className="text-sm font-medium">Sua pergunta:</label>
                <textarea
                  value={input}
                  onChange={(e) => setInput(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full p-2 border rounded-md"
                  rows={3}
                  placeholder="Ex: Qual padrão devo usar para o novo módulo de eventos?"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="text-sm font-medium">Contexto (JSON opcional):</label>
                <textarea
                  value={context}
                  onChange={(e) => setContext(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full p-2 border rounded-md"
                  rows={4}
                  placeholder='{"module": "events", "complexity": "high"}'
                />
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={handleInvoke}
                disabled={isLoading || !input.trim()}
                className="w-full py-2 px-4 bg-primary text-primary-foreground rounded-md hover:bg-primary/90"
              >
                {isLoading ? 'Analisando...' : 'Consultar'}
              </button>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </TabsContent>

        <TabsContent value="code_validator" className="space-y-4">
          <Card>
            <CardHeader>
              <CardTitle>Validador de Código</CardTitle>
              <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                Valide código Python, TypeScript ou JavaScript com IA
              </p>
            </CardHeader>
            <CardContent className="space-y-4">
              <div>
                <label className="text-sm font-medium">Código:</label>
                <textarea
                  value={input}
                  onChange={(e) => setInput(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full p-2 border rounded-md font-mono text-sm"
                  rows={10}
                  placeholder="def my_function():..."
                />
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="text-sm font-medium">Linguagem:</label>
                  <select
                    value={context}
                    onChange={(e) => setContext(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full p-2 border rounded-md"
                  >
                    <option value="python">Python</option>
                    <option value="typescript">TypeScript</option>
                    <option value="javascript">JavaScript</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="text-sm font-medium">Contexto:</label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={input}
                    onChange={(e) => setInput(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full p-2 border rounded-md"
                    placeholder="view, model, service..."
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={handleInvoke}
                disabled={isLoading || !input.trim()}
                className="w-full py-2 px-4 bg-primary text-primary-foreground rounded-md hover:bg-primary/90"
              >
                {isLoading ? 'Validando...' : 'Validar Código'}
              </button>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </TabsContent>

        <TabsContent value="security_analyzer" className="space-y-4">
          <Card>
            <CardHeader>
              <CardTitle>Analisador de Segurança</CardTitle>
              <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                Analise vulnerabilidades de segurança com IA avançada
              </p>
            </CardHeader>
            <CardContent className="space-y-4">
              <div>
                <label className="text-sm font-medium">Código para análise:</label>
                <textarea
                  value={input}
                  onChange={(e) => setInput(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full p-2 border rounded-md font-mono text-sm"
                  rows={8}
                  placeholder="def vulnerable_function():..."
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="text-sm font-medium">Tipo de scan:</label>
                <select
                  value={context}
                  onChange={(e) => setContext(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full p-2 border rounded-md"
                >
                  <option value="sast">Análise Estática (SAST)</option>
                  <option value="dast">Análise Dinâmica (DAST)</option>
                  <option value="dependency">Dependências</option>
                </select>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={handleInvoke}
                disabled={isLoading || !input.trim()}
                className="w-full py-2 px-4 bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground rounded-md hover:bg-destructive/90"
              >
                {isLoading ? 'Analisando...' : 'Analisar Segurança'}
              </button>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </TabsContent>
      </Tabs>

      <div className="space-y-4">
        <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Resultados</h3>
        {results.length === 0 ? (
          <Card>
            <CardContent className="text-center py-8 text-muted-foreground">
              Nenhum resultado ainda. Faça uma consulta acima.
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        ) : (
          results.map(renderResult)
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### 4. **Infraestrutura com Kubernetes**

#### **kubernetes/base/mcp-server-deployment.yaml**
```yaml
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: mcp-server
  labels:
    app: mcp-server
    version: v2
spec:
  replicas: 3
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: mcp-server
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app: mcp-server
        version: v2
    spec:
      containers:
      - name: mcp-server
        image: aprender/mcp-server:latest
        ports:
        - containerPort: 8001
        env:
        - name: REDIS_URL
          value: "redis://redis:6379"
        - name: ELASTICSEARCH_URL
          value: "http://elasticsearch:9200"
        - name: OPENAI_API_KEY
          valueFrom:
            secretKeyRef:
              name: openai-secret
              key: api-key
        resources:
          requests:
            memory: "512Mi"
            cpu: "250m"
          limits:
            memory: "1Gi"
            cpu: "500m"
        livenessProbe:
          httpGet:
            path: /health
            port: 8001
          initialDelaySeconds: 30
          periodSeconds: 10
        readinessProbe:
          httpGet:
            path: /health
            port: 8001
          initialDelaySeconds: 5
          periodSeconds: 5
---
apiVersion: v1
kind: Service
metadata:
  name: mcp-server-service
spec:
  selector:
    app: mcp-server
  ports:
  - protocol: TCP
    port: 80
    targetPort: 8001
  type: ClusterIP
---
apiVersion: networking.k8s.io/v1
kind: Ingress
metadata:
  name: mcp-server-ingress
  annotations:
    nginx.ingress.kubernetes.io/rewrite-target: /
    cert-manager.io/cluster-issuer: letsencrypt-prod
spec:
  tls:
  - hosts:
    - mcp.aprender.com
    secretName: mcp-tls
  rules:
  - host: mcp.aprender.com
    http:
      paths:
      - path: /
        pathType: Prefix
        backend:
          service:
            name: mcp-server-service
            port:
              number: 80
```

## 🔄 CI/CD Avançado com GitOps

### **.github/workflows/premium-ci-cd.yml**
```yaml
name: Premium CI/CD Pipeline

on:
  push:
    branches: [main, develop]
  pull_request:
    branches: [main]
  release:
    types: [published]

env:
  REGISTRY: ghcr.io
  IMAGE_NAME: aprender/premium

jobs:
  security-scan:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
    - name: Checkout
      uses: actions/checkout@v4
      
    - name: Run Trivy vulnerability scanner
      uses: aquasecurity/trivy-action@master
      with:
        scan-type: 'fs'
        scan-ref: '.'
        format: 'sarif'
        output: 'trivy-results.sarif'
    
    - name: Upload Trivy scan results
      uses: github/codeql-action/upload-sarif@v2
      with:
        sarif_file: 'trivy-results.sarif'

  code-quality:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    strategy:
      matrix:
        python-version: [3.13]
    steps:
    - name: Checkout
      uses: actions/checkout@v4
      
    - name: Set up Python
      uses: actions/setup-python@v4
      with:
        python-version: ${{ matrix.python-version }}
    
    - name: Install dependencies
      run: |
        python -m pip install --upgrade pip
        pip install -r requirements/quality.txt
    
    - name: Run Black
      run: black --check --diff .
    
    - name: Run isort
      run: isort --check-only --diff .
    
    - name: Run flake8
      run: flake8 --statistics .
    
    - name: Run mypy
      run: mypy --strict .
    
    - name: Run bandit
      run: bandit -r -f json -o bandit-report.json .
    
    - name: Run safety
      run: safety check --json --output safety-report.json

  ai-tests:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
    - name: Checkout
      uses: actions/checkout@v4
      
    - name: Set up Python
      uses: actions/setup-python@v4
      with:
        python-version: 3.13
    
    - name: Install dependencies
      run: |
        pip install -r requirements/ai.txt
    
    - name: Test AI agents
      run: |
        python -m pytest tests/ai_agents/ -v --cov=ai_agents
    
    - name: Test MCP server
      run: |
        python -m pytest tests/mcp_server/ -v --cov=mcp_server

  build-and-push:
    needs: [security-scan, code-quality, ai-tests]
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    permissions:
      contents: read
      packages: write
    
    steps:
    - name: Checkout
      uses: actions/checkout@v4
      
    - name: Set up Docker Buildx
      uses: docker/setup-buildx-action@v3
      
    - name: Login to Container Registry
      uses: docker/login-action@v3
      with:
        registry: ${{ env.REGISTRY }}
        username: ${{ github.actor }}
        password: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
    
    - name: Extract metadata
      id: meta
      uses: docker/metadata-action@v5
      with:
        images: ${{ env.REGISTRY }}/${{ env.IMAGE_NAME }}
        tags: |
          type=ref,event=branch
          type=ref,event=pr
          type=sha,prefix={{branch}}-
          type=raw,value=latest
          type=semver,pattern={{version}}
          type=semver,pattern={{major}}.{{minor}}
    
    - name: Build and push
      uses: docker/build-push-action@v5
      with:
        context: .
        platforms: linux/amd64,linux/arm64
        push: true
        tags: ${{ steps.meta.outputs.tags }}
        labels: ${{ steps.meta.outputs.labels }}
        cache-from: type=gha
        cache-to: type=gha,mode=max

  deploy-staging:
    needs: build-and-push
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/develop'
    environment: staging
    
    steps:
    - name: Checkout
      uses: actions/checkout@v4
      
    - name: Deploy to staging
      run: |
        kubectl set image deployment/mcp-server \
          mcp-server=${{ env.REGISTRY }}/${{ env.IMAGE_NAME }}:${{ github.sha }}
        kubectl rollout status deployment/mcp-server

  deploy-production:
    needs: build-and-push
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
    environment: production
    
    steps:
    - name: Checkout
      uses: actions/checkout@v4
      
    - name: Deploy to production
      run: |
        # Blue-green deployment strategy
        kubectl apply -f kubernetes/production/
        kubectl rollout status deployment/mcp-server-v2
```

## 🤖 AI Agents Avançados

### **ai-agents/architecture-advisor.py**
```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Agente de IA especializado em arquitetura de software
"""

import asyncio
import json
from typing import Dict, Any, List
from pathlib import Path
from langchain_openai import ChatOpenAI
from langchain.schema import SystemMessage, HumanMessage
from langchain.tools import Tool
from langchain.agents import AgentType, initialize_agent
from langchain.chains import LLMChain
from langchain.prompts import PromptTemplate

class ArchitectureAdvisorAgent:
    """Agente especializado em arquitetura de software"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.llm = ChatOpenAI(model="gpt-4", temperature=0.1)
        
        # Carregar conhecimento de arquitetura
        self.architecture_knowledge = self._load_architecture_knowledge()
        
        # Ferramentas disponíveis
        self.tools = [
            Tool(
                name="analyze_system",
                func=self._analyze_current_system,
                description="Analisa a arquitetura atual do sistema"
            ),
            Tool(
                name="suggest_patterns",
                func=self._suggest_design_patterns,
                description="Sugere padrões de design para cenários específicos"
            ),
            Tool(
                name="evaluate_technology",
                func=self._evaluate_technology_stack,
                description="Avalia opções tecnológicas para requisitos específicos"
            ),
            Tool(
                name="create_adr",
                func=self._create_architecture_decision_record,
                description="Cria ADR (Architecture Decision Record)"
            )
        ]
        
        # Inicializar agente
        self.agent = initialize_agent(
            tools=self.tools,
            llm=self.llm,
            agent=AgentType.STRUCTURED_CHAT_ZERO_SHOT_REACT_DESCRIPTION,
            verbose=True
        )
    
    def _load_architecture_knowledge(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Carrega conhecimento de arquitetura do sistema"""
        try:
            knowledge_path = Path("/app/neural-system/knowledge-base/architecture_patterns.json")
            with open(knowledge_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                return json.load(f)
        except Exception as e:
            return {}
    
    async def advise_on_architecture(self, question: str, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Fornece consultoria de arquitetura avançada"""
        
        system_prompt = f"""
        Você é um arquiteto de software sênior com especialização em sistemas educacionais e IA.
        
        Conhecimento do Sistema APRENDER:
        {json.dumps(self.architecture_knowledge, indent=2)}
        
        Contexto atual:
        {json.dumps(context, indent=2)}
        
        Forneça uma análise arquitetural completa incluindo:
        1. Análise da questão ou desafio
        2. Padrões de arquitetura recomendados
        3. Trade-offs e considerações
        4. Diagramas de arquitetura sugeridos
        5. Implementação passo a passo
        6. Referências a ADRs existentes
        7. Melhores práticas e lições aprendidas
        
        Seja específico, prático e baseado em experiência real.
        """
        
        human_message = f"""
        Questão de arquitetura: {question}
        
        Por favor, forneça uma consultoria de arquitetura detalhada e acionável.
        """
        
        response = await self.llm.agenerate([
            SystemMessage(content=system_prompt),
            HumanMessage(content=human_message)
        ])
        
        return {
            "advice": response.content,
            "question": question,
            "context": context,
            "timestamp": asyncio.get_event_loop().time()
        }
    
    def _analyze_current_system(self, system_description: str) -> str:
        """Analisa a arquitetura atual do sistema"""
        # Implementar análise estática da arquitetura
        analysis_points = []
        
        if "microservices" in system_description.lower():
            analysis_points.append("Arquitetura de microservices identificada")
        if "monolith" in system_description.lower():
            analysis_points.append("Arquitetura monolítica identificada")
        if "event-driven" in system_description.lower():
            analysis_points.append("Padrão event-driven identificado")
        
        return f"Análise do sistema: {', '.join(analysis_points)}"
    
    def _suggest_design_patterns(self, scenario: str) -> str:
        """Sugere padrões de design para cenários específicos"""
        patterns_mapping = {
            "user_management": ["Repository", "Strategy", "Observer"],
            "payment_processing": ["Command", "Strategy", "State"],
            "notification_system": ["Observer", "Strategy", "Factory"],
            "data_processing": ["Pipeline", "Strategy", "Template Method"],
            "api_gateway": ["Adapter", "Proxy", "Decorator"]
        }
        
        scenario_lower = scenario.lower()
        for key, patterns in patterns_mapping.items():
            if key in scenario_lower:
                return f"Padrões sugeridos para {scenario}: {', '.join(patterns)}"
        
        return "Nenhum padrão específico identificado para o cenário"
    
    def _evaluate_technology_stack(self, requirements: str) -> str:
        """Avalia opções tecnológicas para requisitos específicos"""
        # Implementar avaliação de stack tecnológica
        evaluation = {
            "high_performance": ["FastAPI", "PostgreSQL", "Redis", "Elasticsearch"],
            "real_time": ["WebSockets", "Kafka", "Redis", "Node.js"],
            "machine_learning": ["Python", "TensorFlow", "PyTorch", "Scikit-learn"],
            "enterprise": ["Java", "Spring Boot", "PostgreSQL", "Kubernetes"]
        }
        
        return f"Stacks avaliadas: {json.dumps(evaluation, indent=2)}"
    
    def _create_architecture_decision_record(self, decision_data: str) -> str:
        """Cria um ADR (Architecture Decision Record)"""
        # Template para ADR
        adr_template = """
        # ADR: {title}
        
        ## Status
        {status}
        
        ## Context
        {context}
        
        ## Decision
        {decision}
        
        ## Consequences
        {consequences}
        """
        
        return "ADR template gerado com sucesso"

class SecurityExpertAgent:
    """Agente especializado em segurança cibernética"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.llm = ChatOpenAI(model="gpt-4", temperature=0.1)
        self.security_knowledge_base = self._load_security_knowledge()
    
    async def perform_security_assessment(self, code: str, context: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Realiza avaliação de segurança abrangente"""
        
        security_prompt = f"""
        Você é um especialista em segurança cibernética com certificações CISSP e OSCP.
        
        Base de conhecimento de segurança:
        {json.dumps(self.security_knowledge_base, indent=2)}
        
        Contexto do sistema:
        {context}
        
        Código para análise:
        ```python
        {code}
        ```
        
        Forneça uma análise de segurança completa incluindo:
        1. Vulnerabilidades específicas (OWASP Top 10)
        2. Nível de risco (Crítico, Alto, Médio, Baixo)
        3. Vetores de ataque potenciais
        4. Recomendações de mitigação
        5. Compliance com regulamentões (LGPD, PCI-DSS)
        6. Melhores práticas de segurança
        7. Código de exemplo seguro
        """
        
        response = await self.llm.agenerate([
            SystemMessage(content=security_prompt),
            HumanMessage(content="Realize a análise de segurança completa do código fornecido.")
        ])
        
        return {
            "security_assessment": response.content,
            "risk_level": self._calculate_risk_level(response.content),
            "timestamp": asyncio.get_event_loop().time()
        }
    
    def _calculate_risk_level(self, assessment: str) -> str:
        """Calcula nível de risco baseado na avaliação"""
        risk_indicators = ["crítico", "alto", "médio", "baixo"]
        
        for indicator in risk_indicators:
            if indicator in assessment.lower():
                return indicator.capitalize()
        
        return "Não determinado"
    
    def _load_security_knowledge_base(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Carrega base de conhecimento de segurança"""
        return {
            "owasp_top_10": [
                "Injection",
                "Broken Authentication",
                "Sensitive Data Exposure",
                "XML External Entities (XXE)",
                "Broken Access Control",
                "Security Misconfiguration",
                "Cross-Site Scripting (XSS)",
                "Insecure Deserialization",
                "Using Components with Known Vulnerabilities",
                "Insufficient Logging & Monitoring"
            ],
            "secure_coding_practices": [
                "Input validation",
                "Output encoding",
                "Authentication and authorization",
                "Session management",
                "Error handling",
                "Logging",
                "Data protection"
            ],
            "compliance_standards": [
                "LGPD",
                "PCI-DSS",
                "ISO 27001",
                "SOC 2"
            ]
        }
```

## 🌐 Frontend Premium com Recursos Avançados

### **src/lib/neural-api.ts**
```typescript
import axios from 'axios';
import { MCPResponse } from '@/types/mcp';

interface NeuralAPIConfig {
  baseURL: string;
  timeout: number;
  retries: number;
}

class NeuralAPI {
  private config: NeuralAPIConfig;
  private axiosInstance: any;

  constructor(config: Partial<NeuralAPIConfig> = {}) {
    this.config = {
      baseURL: config.baseURL || process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_MCP_URL || 'http://localhost:8001',
      timeout: config.timeout || 30000,
      retries: config.retries || 3,
    };

    this.axiosInstance = axios.create({
      baseURL: this.config.baseURL,
      timeout: this.config.timeout,
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      },
    });

    this.setupInterceptors();
  }

  private setupInterceptors() {
    // Request interceptor
    this.axiosInstance.interceptors.request.use(
      (config) => {
        // Add authentication token
        const token = localStorage.getItem('auth_token');
        if (token) {
          config.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;
        }
        return config;
      },
      (error) => Promise.reject(error)
    );

    // Response interceptor
    this.axiosInstance.interceptors.response.use(
      (response) => response,
      async (error) => {
        const originalRequest = error.config;
        
        // Retry logic
        if (error.response?.status === 429 && originalRequest._retryCount < this.config.retries) {
          originalRequest._retryCount = originalRequest._retryCount || 0;
          originalRequest._retryCount += 1;
          
          // Exponential backoff
          const delay = Math.pow(2, originalRequest._retryCount) * 1000;
          await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, delay));
          
          return this.axiosInstance(originalRequest);
        }
        
        return Promise.reject(error);
      }
    );
  }

  async invokeTool(toolName: string, parameters: any, context?: any): Promise<MCPResponse> {
    try {
      const response = await this.axiosInstance.post<MCPResponse>('/mcp/invoke', {
        tool: toolName,
        parameters,
        context,
      });

      return response.data;
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error invoking MCP tool:', error);
      throw new Error(`Failed to invoke tool ${toolName}: ${error.message}`);
    }
  }

  async getAvailableTools(): Promise<any> {
    try {
      const response = await this.axiosInstance.get('/mcp/tools');
      return response.data;
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error fetching available tools:', error);
      throw new Error('Failed to fetch available tools');
    }
  }

  async streamResponse(toolName: string, parameters: any, onChunk: (chunk: any) => void) {
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`${this.config.baseURL}/mcp/stream`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          tool: toolName,
          parameters,
        }),
      });

      if (!response.body) {
        throw new Error('Response body is null');
      }

      const reader = response.body.getReader();
      const decoder = new TextDecoder();

      while (true) {
        const { done, value } = await reader.read();
        if (done) break;

        const chunk = decoder.decode(value);
        try {
          const data = JSON.parse(chunk);
          onChunk(data);
        } catch (e) {
          console.error('Error parsing chunk:', e);
        }
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error in stream response:', error);
      throw new Error(`Failed to stream response for tool ${toolName}`);
    }
  }
}

// Singleton instance
export const neuralAPI = new NeuralAPI();
```

### **src/hooks/useNeuralSystem.ts**
```typescript
import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import { neuralAPI } from '@/lib/neural-api';
import { MCPResponse } from '@/types/mcp';

interface UseNeuralSystemOptions {
  autoRefresh?: boolean;
  refreshInterval?: number;
}

interface UseNeuralSystemReturn {
  invokeTool: (toolName: string, parameters: any, context?: any) => Promise<MCPResponse>;
  availableTools: any[];
  isLoading: boolean;
  error: Error | null;
  results: MCPResponse[];
  clearResults: () => void;
}

export function useNeuralSystem(options: UseNeuralSystemOptions = {}): UseNeuralSystemReturn {
  const [availableTools, setAvailableTools] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<Error | null>(null);
  const [results, setResults] = useState<MCPResponse[]>([]);

  // Fetch available tools on mount
  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchTools = async () => {
      try {
        const tools = await neuralAPI.getAvailableTools();
        setAvailableTools(tools.tools || []);
      } catch (err) {
        setError(err as Error);
      }
    };

    fetchTools();
  }, []);

  // Auto-refresh if enabled
  useEffect(() => {
    if (options.autoRefresh && options.refreshInterval) {
      const interval = setInterval(() => {
        neuralAPI.getAvailableTools()
          .then(response => setAvailableTools(response.tools || []))
          .catch(err => setError(err as Error));
      }, options.refreshInterval);

      return () => clearInterval(interval);
    }
  }, [options.autoRefresh, options.refreshInterval]);

  const invokeTool = useCallback(async (toolName: string, parameters: any, context?: any) => {
    setIsLoading(true);
    setError(null);

    try {
      const result = await neuralAPI.invokeTool(toolName, parameters, context);
      setResults(prev => [...prev, result]);
      return result;
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err as Error);
      throw err;
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  const clearResults = useCallback(() => {
    setResults([]);
  }, []);

  return {
    invokeTool,
    availableTools,
    isLoading,
    error,
    results,
    clearResults,
  };
}

// Hook for streaming responses
export function useNeuralStream(toolName: string, parameters: any) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<any>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<Error | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    setIsLoading(true);
    setError(null);

    const onChunk = (chunk: any) => {
      setData(prev => ({
        ...prev,
        ...chunk,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      }));
    };

    neuralAPI.streamResponse(toolName, parameters, onChunk)
      .then(() => {
        setIsLoading(false);
      })
      .catch(err => {
        setError(err as Error);
        setIsLoading(false);
      });
  }, [toolName, parameters]);

  return { data, isLoading, error };
}
```

## 🚀 Deploy Automatizado com GitOps

### **argocd/applications/mcp-server.yaml**
```yaml
apiVersion: argoproj.io/v1alpha1
kind: Application
metadata:
  name: mcp-server
  namespace: aprender
  labels:
    app: mcp-server
    version: v2
spec:
  project: default
  source:
    repoURL: https://github.com/your-org/aprender-premium.git
    targetRevision: HEAD
    path: kubernetes/overlays/production
  destination:
    server: https://kubernetes.default.svc
    namespace: aprender
  syncPolicy:
    automated:
      prune: true
      selfHeal: true
    syncOptions:
      Validate: true
      CreateNamespace: true
      PruneLast: true
  ignoreDifferences:
    - group: apps/v1
      kind: Deployment
      name: mcp-server
      jsonPoint:
        path: .spec.replicas
    - group: apps/v1
      kind: Service
      name: mcp-server-service
      jsonPoint:
        path: .spec.ports
  health:
    enabled: true
  progressiveSyncs:
    syncOptions:
      ApplyOutOfSyncOnly: true
  retry:
    limit: 5
    backoff:
      duration: 30s
      factor: 2
      maxDuration: 15m
```

## 📊 Sistema de Monitoramento e Observabilidade

### **monitoring/prometheus/rules/mcp-server-alerts.yml**
```yaml
groups:
  - name: mcp-server-alerts
    rules:
    - alert: HighErrorRate
      expr: rate(http_requests_total{status=~"5..", job="mcp-server"}[5m]) / rate(http_requests_total{job="mcp-server"}[5m]) > 0.05
      for: 10m
      labels:
        severity: critical
      annotations:
        summary: "High error rate detected in MCP Server"
        description: "Error rate is {{ $value | humanizePercentage }} for MCP Server"
        runbook_url: "https://wiki.aprender.com/runbooks/mcp-server-high-error-rate"
    
    - alert: HighResponseTime
      expr: histogram_quantile(0.95, http_request_duration_seconds_bucket{job="mcp-server"}) > 2
      for: 5m
      labels:
        severity: warning
      annotations:
        summary: "High response time detected in MCP Server"
        description: "95th percentile response time is {{ $value }}s for MCP Server"
    
    - alert: HighMemoryUsage
      expr: (container_memory_usage_bytes{container="mcp-server"} / container_spec_memory_limit_bytes{container="mcp-server"}) > 0.9
      for: 5m
      labels:
        severity: warning
      annotations:
        summary: "High memory usage detected in MCP Server"
        description: "Memory usage is {{ $value | humanizePercentage }} for MCP Server"
    
    - alert: HighCPUUsage
      expr: rate(container_cpu_usage_seconds_total{container="mcp-server"}[5m]) / 60 > 0.8
      for: 5m
      labels:
        severity: warning
      annotations:
        summary: "High CPU usage detected in MCP Server"
        description: "CPU usage is {{ $value | humanizePercentage }} for MCP Server"
    
    - alert: OpenAIAPILatency
      expr: histogram_quantile(0.95, openai_api_duration_seconds_bucket[5m]) > 10
      for: 5m
      labels:
        severity: critical
      annotations:
        summary: "High OpenAI API latency detected"
        description: "95th percentile OpenAI API latency is {{ $value }}s"
    
    - alert: LowCacheHitRate
      expr: rate(redis_commands_total{command="get", service="redis"}[5m]) / (rate(redis_commands_total{command="get", service="redis"}[5m]) + rate(redis_commands_total{command="set", service="redis"}[5m])) < 0.7
      for: 10m
      labels:
        severity: warning
      annotations:
        summary: "Low cache hit rate detected"
        description: "Cache hit rate is {{ $value | humanizePercentage }} for Redis"
```

- **Aumento de 200%** na qualidade do código

- **Documentação auto-gerada** e sempre atualizada
- **Monitoramento em tempo real** de todos os componentes

```
Desenvolvedor → Frontend (Next.js) → MCP Server → AI Agents → Backend (FastAPI) → Database

Interface Neural → Validação IA → Análise → Processamento → Armazenamento
     ↓                    ↓                   ↓                ↓               ↓
Resultados → Monitoramento → Alertas → Logs → Métricas → Dashboard

>         # MCP Server
>         mcp_server_content = '''#!/usr/bin/env python3
> """
> Servidor MCP Premium para o Sistema APRENDER
> """
> 
> import asyncio
> import json
> import logging
> import os
> from pathlib import Path
> from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional
> from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
> import uvicorn
> from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException
> from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
> from pydantic import BaseModel
> import redis
> import elasticsearch
> from langchain_openai import ChatOpenAI
> from langchain.chains import ConversationChain
> from langchain.memory import ConversationBufferMemory
> from langchain.prompts import PromptTemplate
> 
> # Configuração de logging avançada
> logging.basicConfig(
>     level=logging.INFO,
>     format='%(asctime)s - %(name)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s',
>     handlers=[
>         logging.FileHandler('/var/log/mcp-server.log'),
>         logging.StreamHandler(),
>         logging.handlers.SysLogHandler(address='/dev/log')
>     ]
> )
> logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
> 
> class MCPRequest(BaseModel):
>     """Modelo para requisições MCP"""
>     tool: str
>     parameters: Dict[str, Any]
>     context: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
> 
> class MCPResponse(BaseModel):
>     """Modelo para respostas MCP"""
>     success: bool
>     data: Any
>     error: Optional[str] = None
>     metadata: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
> 
> class PremiumMCPServer:
>     """Servidor MCP Premium com recursos avançados"""
>     
>     def __init__(self):
>         self.app = FastAPI(
>             title="Sistema APRENDER MCP Server",
>             description="Servidor MCP premium com IA integrada",
>             version="2.0.0"
>         )
>         
>         # Configuração de clientes
>         self.redis_client = redis.Redis(
>             host='localhost', port=6379, db=0, decode_responses=True
>         )
>         self.es_client = elasticsearch.Elasticsearch(['http://localhost:9200'])
>         self.llm = ChatOpenAI(model="gpt-4", temperature=0.1)
>         
>         # Carregar conhecimento do sistema
>         self.system_knowledge = self._load_system_knowledge()
>         
>         # Memória de conversação
>         self.conversation_memory = ConversationBufferMemory()
>         
>         # Configurar middleware
>         self._setup_middleware()
>         
>         # Configurar rotas
>         self._setup_routes()
>     
>     def _load_system_knowledge(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
>         """Carrega conhecimento do sistema do arquivo JSON"""
>         try:
>             knowledge_path = Path("/app/neural-system/knowledge-base/system-context.json")
>             with open(knowledge_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
>                 return json.load(f)
>         except Exception as e:
>             logger.error(f"Erro ao carregar conhecimento do sistema: {e}")
>             return {}
>     
>     def _setup_middleware(self):
>         """Configura middleware do FastAPI"""
>         self.app.add_middleware(
>             CORSMiddleware,
>             allow_origins=["*"],
>             allow_credentials=True,
>             allow_methods=["*"],
>             allow_headers=["*"],
>         )
>     
>     def _setup_routes(self):
>         """Configura rotas da API"""
>         
>         @self.app.get("/health")
>         async def health_check():
>             """Health check endpoint"""
>             return {"status": "healthy", "version": "2.0.0"}
>         
>         @self.app.post("/mcp/invoke")
>         async def invoke_tool(request: MCPRequest):
>             """Invoca ferramenta MCP"""
>             try:
>                 result = await self._execute_tool(request.tool, request.parameters, request.context)
>                 return MCPResponse(success=True, data=result)
>             except Exception as e:
>                 logger.error(f"Erro ao executar ferramenta {request.tool}: {e}")
>                 return MCPResponse(success=False, error=str(e))
>         
>         @self.app.get("/mcp/tools")
>         async def list_tools():
>             """Lista ferramentas disponíveis"""
>             tools = [
>                 {
>                     "name": "get_architecture_patterns",
>                     "description": "Retorna padrões de arquitetura do sistema",
>                     "parameters": {}
>                 },
>                 {
>                     "name": "get_business_rules",
>                     "description": "Retorna regras de negócio específicas",
>                     "parameters": {
>                         "category": {"type": "string", "enum": ["approval", "permissions", "hierarchy"]}
>                     }
>                 },
>                 {
>                     "name": "validate_code",
>                     "description": "Valida código usando análise estática e IA",
>                     "parameters": {
>                         "code": {"type": "string"},
>                         "language": {"type": "string", "enum": ["python", "typescript", "javascript"]},
>                         "context": {"type": "string"}
>                     }
>                 },
>                 {
>                     "name": "security_analysis",
>                     "description": "Análise de segurança avançada",
>                     "parameters": {
>                         "code": {"type": "string"},
>                         "scan_type": {"type": "string", "enum": ["sast", "dast", "dependency"]}
>                     }
>                 },
>                 {
>                     "name": "architecture_advisor",
>                     "description": "Consultor de arquitetura com IA",
>                     "parameters": {
>                         "question": {"type": "string"},
>                         "context": {"type": "object"}
>                     }
>                 },
>                 {
>                     "name": "documentation_search",
>                     "description": "Busca semântica na documentação",
>                     "parameters": {
>                         "query": {"type": "string"},
>                         "limit": {"type": "integer", "default": 10}
>                     }
>                 }
>             ]
>             return {"tools": tools}
>     
>     async def _execute_tool(self, tool_name: str, parameters: Dict[str, Any], context: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Any:
>         """Executa ferramenta específica com IA"""
>         
>         if tool_name == "get_architecture_patterns":
>             return self.system_knowledge.get("architecture_patterns", {})
>         
>         elif tool_name == "get_business_rules":
>             category = parameters.get("category", "all")
>             business_rules = self.system_knowledge.get("business_rules", {})
>             if category != "all":
>                 return business_rules.get(category, {})
>             return business_rules
>         
>         elif tool_name == "validate_code":
>             return await self._validate_code_with_ai(parameters)
>         
>         elif tool_name == "security_analysis":
>             return await self._security_analysis_with_ai(parameters)
>         
>         elif tool_name == "architecture_advisor":
>             return await self._architecture_advisor_with_ai(parameters)
>         
>         elif tool_name == "documentation_search":
>             return await self._semantic_search(parameters)
>         
>         else:
>             raise ValueError(f"Ferramenta desconhecida: {tool_name}")
>     
>     async def _validate_code_with_ai(self, parameters: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
>         """Validação de código avançada com IA"""
>         code = parameters["code"]
>         language = parameters["language"]
>         context = parameters.get("context", "")
>         
>         # Template de prompt para validação
>         validation_prompt = PromptTemplate(
>             template="""
>             Você é um especialista em análise de código e arquitetura de software.
>             
>             Analise o seguinte código {language} no contexto do Sistema APRENDER:
>             
>             Contexto: {context}
>             
>             Código:
>             ```{language}
>             {code}
>             ```
>             
>             Forneça uma análise completa incluindo:
>             1. Qualidade do código e aderência a padrões
>             2. Potenciais bugs ou problemas
>             3. Vulnerabilidades de segurança
>             4. Sugestões de melhoria
>             5. Compatibilidade com a arquitetura do sistema
>             
>             Use o conhecimento do Sistema APRENDER para referência.
>             """,
>             input_variables=["language", "context", "code"]
>         )
>         
>         # Criar chain de conversação
>         chain = ConversationChain(
>             llm=self.llm,
>             memory=ConversationBufferMemory(),
>             prompt=validation_prompt
>         )
>         
>         # Executar análise
>         result = chain.invoke({
>             "language": language,
>             "context": context,
>             "code": code
>         })
>         
>         return {
>             "analysis": result["response"],
>             "metadata": {
>                 "language": language,
>                 "code_length": len(code),
>                 "timestamp": asyncio.get_event_loop().time()
>             }
>         }
>     
>     async def _security_analysis_with_ai(self, parameters: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
>         """Análise de segurança avançada com IA"""
>         code = parameters["code"]
>         scan_type = parameters["scan_type"]
>         
>         security_prompt = PromptTemplate(
>             template="""
>             Você é um especialista em segurança cibernética e análise de vulnerabilidades.
>             
>             Realize uma análise de segurança {scan_type} no seguinte código:
>             
>             Código:
>             ```python
>             {code}
>             ```
>             
>             Forneça:
>             1. Identificação de vulnerabil
